"use client";

import { DashboardComposer } from '@/components/dashboard/DashboardComposer';
import { useAuth } from '@/components/layout/auth-provider';
import { useGetDashboard } from '@/hooks/api/useDashboard';

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const orgIdNum = activeOrgId ? Number(activeOrgId) : undefined;
  
  const { data: dashboardData, isLoading, isError } = useGetDashboard(orgIdNum);

  if (isLoading || !dashboardData) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center animate-pulse text-zinc-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">
        Fetching Live Telemetry...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (isError) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-red-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">
        Failed to load telemetry data.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DashboardComposer 
      userName={dashboardData.userName}
      stats={dashboardData.stats}
      recentPlans={dashboardData.recentPlans.map(rp => ({ ...rp, id: String(rp.id) }))}
      healthData={dashboardData.healthData}
      systemEvents={dashboardData.systemEvents.map(evt => ({ ...evt, id: String(evt.id) }))}
      routingActivity={dashboardData.routingActivity}
    />
  );
}

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Inter } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";

// Providers
import { AuthProvider } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { QueryProvider } from "@/components/layout/query-provider"; // <-- NEW IMPORT
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/layout/theme-provider";
import { SidebarProvider } from "@/components/ui/sidebar";

// Layout Components
import { Header } from "@/components/layout/Header";
import { Sidebar } from "@/components/layout/Sidebar";

const inter = Inter({ subsets: ["latin"] });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "EchoNav",
  description: "Navigate with Confidence - Institutional Command Center",
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <body
        suppressHydrationWarning
        className={`${inter.className} bg-background text-foreground flex h-screen overflow-hidden antialiased`}
      >
        <ThemeProvider
          attribute="class"
          defaultTheme="system"
          enableSystem
          disableTransitionOnChange
        >
          {/* --- INJECT THE QUERY PROVIDER HERE --- */}
          <QueryProvider>
            <AuthProvider>
              <SidebarProvider>
                <Sidebar />
                <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0">
                  <Header />
                  <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 md:p-8">
                    {children}
                  </main>
                </div>
              </SidebarProvider>
            </AuthProvider>
          </QueryProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";

import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardFooter,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";

export default function ResetPasswordPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError("");
    setIsSuccess(false);

    // Mock API delay to test UI state
    setTimeout(() => {
      setIsSuccess(true);
      setEmail("");
      setIsLoading(false);
    }, 1500);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center p-4 bg-linear-to-b from-background to-secondary/20">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 w-full max-w-105 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
        
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center">
          <Link href="/" className="hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity">
            <Image
              src="/EchoNavFullLogo.svg"
              alt="EchoNav Logo"
              width={340}
              height={60}
              priority
              className="dark:invert"
            />
          </Link>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground font-medium tracking-tight">
            Navigate with Confidence.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full empty:hidden">
          {error && (
            <div className="p-3 text-sm text-red-500 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-md text-center -mb-4">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}
          {isSuccess && (
            <div className="p-3 text-sm text-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 rounded-md text-center -mb-4">
              If an account exists, a reset link has been sent to that email.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <Card className="w-full border-border/50 shadow-xl shadow-black/5 mt-4">
          <CardHeader className="space-y-1 text-center">
            <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">
              Reset Password
            </CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              Enter your email and we&apos;ll send you a reset link.
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="email" className="text-left block">
                  Email
                </Label>
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="name@example.com"
                  className="h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 focus-visible:ring-primary/20"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={isLoading}
                />
              </div>
            </CardContent>

            <CardFooter className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4 pt-2">
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isLoading}
                className="mt-2 w-full h-12 text-base font-semibold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-primary/20 hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
              >
                {isLoading ? "Sending link..." : "Send reset link"}
              </Button>

              <Link
                href="/auth?tab=login"
                className="text-sm text-primary hover:underline font-medium text-center"
              >
                Back to login
              </Link>
            </CardFooter>
          </form>
        </Card>

        <p className="text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground px-8">
          Need help? Contact{" "}
          <Link
            href="/support"
            className="underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-primary"
          >
            Support
          </Link>
          .
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\components\auth\ActivateForm.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
    Card,
    CardContent,
    CardDescription,
    CardFooter,
    CardHeader,
    CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { authActions } from "@/lib/actions/auth";
import { CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

export function ActivateForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const urlToken = searchParams.get("token") || "";

  const [token, setToken] = useState(urlToken);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [successRole, setSuccessRole] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const role = await authActions.activateInvite(token, { email, name, password });
      setSuccessRole(role);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to activate account.";
      setError(message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (successRole) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 w-full max-w-md animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center">
          <Image src="/EchoNavFullLogo.svg" alt="EchoNav Logo" width={240} height={40} className="dark:invert mb-4" />
        </div>
        <Card className="w-full border-2 border-emerald-500 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-500/10">
          <CardContent className="p-8 flex flex-col items-center text-center space-y-4">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-16 h-16 text-emerald-500 mb-2" />
            {successRole === "ADMIN" ? (
              <>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-tight">Admin Verified</h3>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-500">Successful login as admin. You may now log in normally to access the command center.</p>
                <Link href="/auth?tab=login" className="w-full mt-4">
                  <Button className="w-full h-12 text-base font-semibold rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 text-white">Go to Login</Button>
                </Link>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-tight">Technician Verified</h3>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-500">Successful account created. Go to the EchoNav mobile app and log in there to access your deployment tasks.</p>
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 w-full max-w-md animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center">
        <Image src="/EchoNavFullLogo.svg" alt="EchoNav Logo" width={240} height={40} className="dark:invert mb-4" />
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="w-full p-3 text-sm text-red-500 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-md text-center">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <Card className="w-full border-border/50 shadow-xl shadow-black/5">
        <CardHeader className="space-y-1 text-center border-b border-border/50 pb-6 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50">
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Activate Account</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Verify your invitation to join the staff roster.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4 pt-6">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Secure Token</Label>
              <Input required value={token} onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value)} disabled={!!urlToken || isLoading} className="font-mono text-xs bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Invited Email</Label>
              <Input type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} className="bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Full Name</Label>
              <Input required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} className="bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Set Password</Label>
              <Input type="password" required value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} className="bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
          <CardFooter className="pb-6">
            <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading} className="w-full h-12 text-base font-semibold rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 text-white shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95">
              {isLoading ? "Verifying..." : "Complete Setup"}
            </Button>
          </CardFooter>
        </form>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { LoginForm } from "./LoginForm";
import { SignupForm } from "./SignupForm";

export function AuthComposer() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const activeTab = searchParams.get("tab") || "login";

  const handleTabChange = (value: string) => {
    router.push(`/auth?tab=${value}`);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-start gap-8 w-full max-w-5xl animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
      
      {/* Header & Logo */}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 text-center">
        <Link href="/" className="hover:opacity-80 transition-opacity">
          <Image
            src="/EchoNavFullLogo.svg"
            alt="EchoNav Logo"
            width={340}
            height={60}
            priority
            className="dark:invert"
          />
        </Link>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground font-medium tracking-tight mt-2">
          Navigate with Confidence.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Tabs Control */}
      <Tabs
        value={activeTab}
        onValueChange={handleTabChange}
        className="w-full flex flex-col items-center"
      >
        <TabsList className="grid w-full max-w-md grid-cols-2 p-1 bg-secondary/50 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl">

            value="login"
            className="rounded-lg data-[state=active]:shadow-sm"

            value="signup"

            Sign Up
          </TabsTrigger>

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardFooter,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import axios from "axios";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";

export function LoginForm() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      // Trigger the real Spring Boot login flow
      await login(email, password);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      // Catch Spring Boot's custom ErrorResponse
      if (axios.isAxiosError(err) && err.response?.data?.message) {
        setError(err.response.data.message);
      } else {
        setError("Unable to connect to the server. Please check your network.");
      }
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
      {/* Local Error Display */}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 text-sm text-red-500 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-md text-center">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <Card className="border-border/50 shadow-xl shadow-black/5">
        <CardHeader className="space-y-1">
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">
            Welcome back
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Enter your credentials to access your dashboard.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="login-email">Email</Label>
              <Input
                id="login-email"
                type="email"
                placeholder="name@example.com"
                className="h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 focus-visible:ring-primary/20"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                disabled={isLoading}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <Label htmlFor="login-password">Password</Label>
                <Link
                  href="/reset-password"
                  className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline font-medium"
                >
                  Forgot password?
                </Link>
              </div>
              <Input
                id="login-password"
                type="password"
                className="mb-3 h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 focus-visible:ring-primary/20"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                disabled={isLoading}
              />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
          <CardFooter className="pt-2">
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="mt-2 w-full h-12 text-base font-semibold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-primary/20 hover:scale-[1.01] active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
            >
              {isLoading ? "Authenticating..." : "Log in"}
            </Button>
          </CardFooter>
        </form>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\components\auth\SignupForm.tsx

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Card,
  CardContent,
  CardDescription,
  CardFooter,
  CardHeader,
  CardTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Command,
  CommandEmpty,
  CommandGroup,
  CommandInput,
  CommandItem,
  CommandList,
} from "@/components/ui/command";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Check, ChevronsUpDown } from "lucide-react";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";

import { useGooglePlacesAutocomplete } from "@/hooks/AutoAddressGoogleMaps";
import { COUNTRIES } from "@/lib/countries";

export function SignupForm() {
  const { register } = useAuth();

  const [instName, setInstName] = useState("");
  const [adminName, setAdminName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [openCountrySelect, setOpenCountrySelect] = useState(false);
  const [selectedCountryCode, setSelectedCountryCode] = useState(COUNTRIES[0].code);
  const [streetAddress, setStreetAddress] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [stateProvince, setStateProvince] = useState("");
  const [postalCode, setPostalCode] = useState("");
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const currentCountry = COUNTRIES.find((c) => c.code === selectedCountryCode);
  const currentDialCode = currentCountry?.dialCode || "";
  const streetInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  
  // Clean string pattern without regex slashes
  const passwordRe = new RegExp("^(?=.*?[A-Z])(?=.*?[a-z])(?=.*?[0-9])(?=.*?[#?!@$%^&*-]).{8,}$");

  useGooglePlacesAutocomplete(streetInputRef, selectedCountryCode, (details) => {
    setStreetAddress(details.streetAddress);
    setCity(details.city);
    setStateProvince(details.stateProvince);
    setPostalCode(details.postalCode);
    if (details.countryCode) {
      const matched = COUNTRIES.find((c) => c.code === details.countryCode);
      if (matched) setSelectedCountryCode(matched.code);
    }
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError("");

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }
    // Corrected logic: trigger error when validation FAILS (!test)
    if (!passwordRe.test(password)) {
      setError("Password must contain an uppercase letter, lowercase letter, number, and special character.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      await register({
        orgName: instName,
        adminName: adminName,
        email: email,
        password: password,
        country: currentCountry?.name || "",
        address: `${streetAddress}, ${city}, ${stateProvince}, ${postalCode}`,
        contactNo: `${currentDialCode}${contactNumber}`,
      });
      
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      if (err instanceof Error) {
        setError(err.message);
      } else {
        setError("An unknown error occurred during registration.");
      }
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-4 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-500">
      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 text-sm text-red-500 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/20 rounded-md text-center">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <Card className="border-border/50 shadow-xl shadow-black/5 overflow-hidden">
        <CardHeader className="bg-secondary/10 pb-6 border-b border-border/50">
          <CardTitle className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Create an account</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Join EchoNav to start navigating with confidence.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <CardContent className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-x-6 gap-y-5 pt-6">
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="inst-name">Institution Name</Label>
              <Input id="inst-name" required value={instName} onChange={(e) => setInstName(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} placeholder="University of Johannesburg" className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="signup-AdminName">Admin Name</Label>
              <Input id="signup-AdminName" required value={adminName} onChange={(e) => setAdminName(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} placeholder="Dr. Jane Doe" className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="signup-email">Admin Email</Label>
              <Input id="signup-email" type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} placeholder="admin@uj.ac.za" className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 pt-2 border-t border-border/50 mt-1">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Location Details</h3>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 flex flex-col">
              <Label>Country / Region</Label>
              <Popover open={openCountrySelect} onOpenChange={setOpenCountrySelect}>
                <PopoverTrigger asChild>
                  <Button variant="outline" disabled={isLoading} className="w-full justify-between h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30">
                    {currentCountry ? currentCountry.name : "Select country..."}
                    <ChevronsUpDown className="ml-2 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 opacity-50" />
                  </Button>
                </PopoverTrigger>
                <PopoverContent className="w-(--radix-popover-trigger-width) p-0" align="start">
                  <Command>
                    <CommandInput placeholder="Search country..." />
                    <CommandList>
                      <CommandEmpty>No country found.</CommandEmpty>
                      <CommandGroup>
                        {COUNTRIES.map((country) => (
                          <CommandItem key={country.code} value={country.name} onSelect={() => { setSelectedCountryCode(country.code); setOpenCountrySelect(false); }}>
                            <Check className={cn("mr-2 h-4 w-4", selectedCountryCode === country.code ? "opacity-100" : "opacity-0")} />
                            {country.name} ({country.dialCode})
                          </CommandItem>
                        ))}
                      </CommandGroup>
                    </CommandList>
                  </Command>
                </PopoverContent>
              </Popover>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="signup-street">Street Address</Label>
              <Input ref={streetInputRef} id="signup-street" required disabled={isLoading} value={streetAddress} onChange={(e) => setStreetAddress(e.target.value)} placeholder="123 University Rd" className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" autoComplete="off" />
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
              <Input placeholder="City" value={city} onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)} required disabled={isLoading} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
              <Input placeholder="State" value={stateProvince} onChange={(e) => setStateProvince(e.target.value)} required disabled={isLoading} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
              <Input placeholder="Zip" value={postalCode} onChange={(e) => setPostalCode(e.target.value)} required disabled={isLoading} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label>Contact Number</Label>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <div className="flex h-10 w-18 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-secondary/50 text-sm font-medium border border-input/50">{currentDialCode}</div>
                <Input type="tel" required value={contactNumber} onChange={(e) => setContactNumber(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} placeholder="11 559 1234" className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="md:col-span-2 pt-2 border-t border-border/50 mt-1">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">Security</h3>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="signup-password">Password</Label>
              <Input id="signup-password" type="password" required value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="confirm-password">Confirm Password</Label>
              <Input id="confirm-password" type="password" required value={confirmPassword} onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} disabled={isLoading} className="h-10 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30" />
            </div>
          </CardContent>
          <CardFooter className="bg-secondary/5 pt-4 pb-6 mt-4 border-t border-border/50">
            <Button type="submit" disabled={isLoading} className="mt-2 w-full h-12 text-base font-semibold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-primary/20 transition-all">
              {isLoading ? "Provisioning Organization..." : "Create account"}
            </Button>
          </CardFooter>
        </form>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { BatteryWarning, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Cell, Pie, PieChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip } from "recharts";

export interface CriticalBeacon {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  battery: string;
}

export interface HardwareHealthData {
  total: number;
  healthyCount: number;
  warningCount: number;
  criticalCount: number;
  criticalItems: CriticalBeacon[];
}

const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 2;
const textureStyle = {
  backgroundImage:
    "repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, currentColor 0, currentColor 1px, transparent 1px, transparent 8px)",
};

export function HardwareHealthChart({
  healthData,
}: {
  healthData: HardwareHealthData;
}) {
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const chartData = [
    {
      name: "Healthy (>50%)",
      value: healthData.healthyCount,
      color: "#3b82f6",
    },
    {
      name: "Warning (15-50%)",
      value: healthData.warningCount,
      color: "#fbbf24",
    },
    {
      name: "Critical (<15%)",
      value: healthData.criticalCount,
      color: "#f43f5e",
    },
  ];

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(healthData.criticalItems.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE),
  );
  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE;
  const currentItems = healthData.criticalItems.slice(
    startIndex,
    startIndex + ITEMS_PER_PAGE,
  );

  return (
    <Card className="relative flex flex-col rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 opacity-[0.03] text-zinc-950 dark:text-white pointer-events-none"
        style={textureStyle}
      />

      <CardHeader className="relative z-10 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-sky-300 dark:bg-sky-900 px-5 py-3 shrink-0">
        <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm text-sky-1050 dark:text-sky-100">
          Hardware Health
        </CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="relative z-10 p-5 flex flex-col items-center">
        <div className="h-40 w-full relative mb-4">
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
            <PieChart>
              <Pie
                data={chartData}
                cx="50%"
                cy="50%"
                innerRadius={50}
                outerRadius={70}
                paddingAngle={2}
                dataKey="value"
                stroke="none"
              >
                {chartData.map((entry, index) => (
                  <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.color} />
                ))}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip
                contentStyle={{
                  backgroundColor: "#18181b",
                  color: "#fff",
                  border: "none",
                  borderRadius: "8px",
                  fontWeight: "bold",
                }}
              />
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none flex-col">
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
              {healthData.total}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
              Beacons
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="w-full space-y-2 border-t-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pt-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <h4 className="text-[11px] font-black text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-500 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <BatteryWarning className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Critical (
              {healthData.criticalItems.length})
            </h4>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="icon"
                className="h-5 w-5 rounded border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900"
                onClick={() => setCurrentPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
                disabled={currentPage === 1}
              >
                <ChevronLeft className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white" />
              </Button>
              <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-zinc-500 w-4 text-center">
                {currentPage}
              </span>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="icon"
                className="h-5 w-5 rounded border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900"
                onClick={() =>
                  setCurrentPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))
                }
                disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
              >
                <ChevronRight className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white" />
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-1.5 min-h-17">
            {currentItems.length > 0 ? (
              currentItems.map((item) => (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  className="text-[10px] font-bold flex justify-between bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/20 p-2 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-900/30 text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200"
                >
                  <span className="truncate pr-2">{item.label}</span>
                  <span className="text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">
                    {item.battery}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))
            ) : (
              <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-center p-2 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-500">
                All systems optimal.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

                      Open Editor <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3" />

          ))}

        <div className="absolute -top-10 right-12 flex gap-2">
          <CarouselPrevious className="relative top-0 right-0 left-0 transform-none h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white  dark:shadow-none hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800" />
          <CarouselNext className="relative top-0 right-0 left-0 transform-none h-8 w-8 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white  dark:shadow-none hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800" />
        </div>

import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import React from 'react';

export interface SystemEvent {
  id: string;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'RESOLVED';
  hazardType: string;
  nodeLabel: string;
  buildingName: string;
  floorPlanName: string; 
  timestamp: string;
}

export function SystemEventsFeed({ events }: { events: SystemEvent[] }) {
  return (
    <Card className="relative h-full flex flex-col rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
      
      <CardHeader className="relative z-10 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-900 px-5 py-3 shrink-0">
        <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm text-rose-950 dark:text-rose-100">
          Recent Hazards Log
        </CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      
      <CardContent className="relative z-10 flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5">
        <div className="space-y-5">
            {events.length > 0 ? (
              events.map((event, index) => {
                const isLast = index === events.length - 1;
                const isActive = event.status === 'ACTIVE';
                
                return (
                  <div key={event.id} className={`flex items-start gap-4 ${!isLast ? 'border-b-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-5' : ''}`}>
                      <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-lg border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${isActive ? 'bg-amber-100 border-amber-900 text-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:text-amber-500' : 'bg-emerald-100 border-emerald-900 text-emerald-900 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-500'}`}>
                          {isActive ? <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5" /> : <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                          <p className="text-sm font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                            {isActive ? `Hazard - ${event.hazardType} - ${event.timestamp}` : `Hazard resolved - ${event.hazardType} - ${event.timestamp}`}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                            {event.nodeLabel} - {event.buildingName} - {event.floorPlanName}
                          </p>
                      </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            ) : (
              <div className="text-center py-8 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
                No active hazards
              </div>
            )}
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\components\fleet\AssignmentEngine.tsx
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { useCreateTasks } from "@/hooks/api/useFleet";
import { useGetBuildings, useGetFloorPlans, useGetSpatialGraph } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";
import { Loader2, Map, MapPin, Send, User, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

export interface UnassignedNode {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

interface AssignmentProps {
  nodes?: UnassignedNode[];
  staff: { id: string; name: string }[];
  activeNodeIds?: number[];
}

export function AssignmentEngine({ staff, activeNodeIds = [] }: AssignmentProps) {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const orgIdNum = activeOrgId ? Number(activeOrgId) : undefined;

  const { data: buildings } = useGetBuildings(orgIdNum);
  const firstBuildingId = buildings?.[0]?.id;
  const { data: floorPlans, isLoading: isFloorPlansLoading } = useGetFloorPlans(firstBuildingId);

  const [selectedFloorPlanId, setSelectedFloorPlanId] = useState<string>("");
  const [selectedNodes, setSelectedNodes] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [selectedStaffId, setSelectedStaffId] = useState<string>("");

  const { data: graphData, isLoading: isGraphLoading } = useGetSpatialGraph(
    selectedFloorPlanId ? Number(selectedFloorPlanId) : undefined
  );

  const createTasksMutation = useCreateTasks();

  const dynamicNodes: UnassignedNode[] = (graphData?.nodes || [])
    .filter((node) => !activeNodeIds.includes(Number(node.id)))
    .map((node) => ({
      id: String(node.id),
      label: node.label || `Node ${node.id}`,
      x: node.x,
      y: node.y,
    }));

  const handleFloorPlanChange = (val: string) => {
    setSelectedFloorPlanId(val);
    setSelectedNodes(new Set());
  };

  const toggleNode = (id: string) => {
    const newSelected = new Set(selectedNodes);
    if (newSelected.has(id)) newSelected.delete(id);
    else newSelected.add(id);
    setSelectedNodes(newSelected);
  };

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (selectedNodes.size === dynamicNodes.length && dynamicNodes.length > 0) {
      setSelectedNodes(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedNodes(new Set(dynamicNodes.map((n) => n.id)));
    }
  };

  const handleAssign = async () => {
    if (selectedNodes.size === 0 || !selectedStaffId) return;

    try {
      const nodeIds = Array.from(selectedNodes).map(Number);
      const staffId = Number(selectedStaffId);

      await createTasksMutation.mutateAsync({
        nodeIds,
        staffId,
      });

      setSelectedNodes(new Set());
      setSelectedStaffId("");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      alert("Failed to dispatch tasks. Check console for details.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col">
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black uppercase tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
            <Send className="h-6 w-6 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
            Assignment Workspace
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mt-1">Select location, pick nodes, and assign a technician.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3-Column Grid for step-by-step selection */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 flex-1 min-h-0">
        
        {/* Column 1: Floor Plans */}
        <Card className="flex flex-col border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
          <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 py-4">
            <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
              <Map className="h-4 w-4" /> Step 1: Location
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {isFloorPlansLoading && <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-zinc-500 m-auto mt-4" />}
            {(!floorPlans || floorPlans.length === 0) && !isFloorPlansLoading && (
              <div className="text-center p-4 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest">No Floor Plans Found</div>
            )}
            {floorPlans?.map((fp) => (
              <div
                key={fp.id}
                onClick={() => handleFloorPlanChange(String(fp.id))}
                className={`p-4 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  selectedFloorPlanId === String(fp.id)
                    ? "border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400"
                    : "border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-300 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="font-black text-sm uppercase">{fp.name}</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">ID: {fp.id}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Column 2: Nodes */}
        <Card className="flex flex-col border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
          <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 py-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                <MapPin className="h-4 w-4" /> Step 2: Nodes
              </CardTitle>
              <button
                onClick={toggleAll}
                disabled={dynamicNodes.length === 0}
                className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-800 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {selectedNodes.size === dynamicNodes.length && dynamicNodes.length > 0 ? "Deselect All" : "Select All"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
            {!selectedFloorPlanId && (
              <div className="text-center p-4 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg mt-4">
                Select a floor plan first
              </div>
            )}
            {isGraphLoading && selectedFloorPlanId && <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-zinc-500 m-auto mt-4" />}
            {!isGraphLoading && dynamicNodes.length === 0 && selectedFloorPlanId && (
              <div className="text-center p-4 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg">
                No unassigned nodes available.
              </div>
            )}
            {dynamicNodes.map((node) => (
              <div
                key={node.id}
                onClick={() => toggleNode(node.id)}
                className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-lg border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  selectedNodes.has(node.id)
                    ? "border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30"
                    : "border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-300 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <Checkbox
                    checked={selectedNodes.has(node.id)}
                    onCheckedChange={() => toggleNode(node.id)}
                    className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 data-[state=checked]:bg-indigo-600 data-[state=checked]:border-indigo-600"
                  />
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-black text-sm uppercase">{node.label}</div>
                    <div className="font-mono text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest">ID: {node.id}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {/* Column 3: Staff & Dispatch */}
        <Card className="flex flex-col border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden relative">
          <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 py-4">
            <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
              <Users className="h-4 w-4" /> Step 3: Technician
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pb-24">
            {staff.map((s) => (
              <div
                key={s.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedStaffId(s.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  selectedStaffId === s.id
                    ? "border-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-400"
                    : "border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-300 dark:hover:border-zinc-700"
                }`}
              >
                <User className="h-5 w-5 opacity-70" />
                <span className="font-black text-sm uppercase break-all">{s.name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
          
          {/* Sticky Summary / Action Footer */}
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-4 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-t-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <Button
              onClick={handleAssign}
              disabled={selectedNodes.size === 0 || !selectedStaffId || createTasksMutation.isPending}
              className="w-full bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-black h-12 tracking-widest uppercase text-xs  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:active:translate-x-0 disabled:active:translate-y-0 disabled:active:"
            >
              {createTasksMutation.isPending ? "Dispatching..." : `Create ${selectedNodes.size} Task${selectedNodes.size !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  useGetOrganizationBeacons,
  useGetOrganizationTasks,
} from "@/hooks/api/useFleet";
import { useGetStaff } from "@/hooks/api/useStaff";
import { BeaconDto, DeploymentTaskDto } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { ArrowLeft, LayoutDashboard, RadioTower, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { AssignmentEngine } from "./AssignmentEngine";
import { HardwareInventory, InventoryItem } from "./HardwareInventory";
import { KanbanBoard, Task } from "./KanbanBoard";

type ViewState = "HUB" | "ASSIGN" | "BOARD" | "INVENTORY";

export function FleetComposer() {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const orgIdNum = activeOrgId ? Number(activeOrgId) : undefined;

  const { data: staffData = [], isLoading: staffLoading } = useGetStaff(orgIdNum);
  const { data: tasksData = [], isLoading: tasksLoading } = useGetOrganizationTasks(orgIdNum);
  const { data: beaconsData = [], isLoading: beaconsLoading } = useGetOrganizationBeacons(orgIdNum);

  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<ViewState>("HUB");

  const isLoading = staffLoading || tasksLoading || beaconsLoading;

  const staffOptions = staffData
    .filter(staff => staff.role === "TECHNICIAN")
    .map((staff) => ({
      id: String(staff.id),
      name: staff.email,
    }));

  const mappedTasks: Task[] = tasksData.map((task: DeploymentTaskDto) => {
    const assignedStaff = staffData.find((s) => s.id === task.staffId);
    return {
      id: String(task.id),
      status: task.status as "TO_DO" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "VERIFIED",
      nodeLabel: `Node ID: ${task.nodeId}`,
      staffName: assignedStaff ? assignedStaff.email : "Unknown Technician",
    };
  });

  // THE FIX: Prevent Ghost Tasks by excluding nodes that have ANY task or beacon history
  const taskNodeIds = tasksData.map((task: DeploymentTaskDto) => Number(task.nodeId));
  const inventoryNodeIds = beaconsData
    .filter((b: BeaconDto) => b.nodeId != null && b.status !== "UNLINKED")
    .map((b: BeaconDto) => Number(b.nodeId));
    
  const unavailableNodeIds = Array.from(new Set([...taskNodeIds, ...inventoryNodeIds]));

  const mappedInventory: InventoryItem[] = beaconsData.map((beacon: BeaconDto) => ({
    id: String(beacon.id),
    nodeLabel: beacon.nodeLabel || `Node ID: ${beacon.nodeId}`,
    beaconId: beacon.uuid,
    rssi: beacon.calibratedRssiBaseline,
    status: beacon.status as "ACTIVE" | "UNLINKED",
  }));

  const activeTasksCount = tasksData.filter((task: DeploymentTaskDto) => task.status === "TO_DO" || task.status === "IN_PROGRESS").length;
  const unlinkedBeaconsCount = mappedInventory.filter((i) => i.status === "UNLINKED").length;

  if (activeView !== "HUB") {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col h-full space-y-6 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-500">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveView("HUB")}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xs text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Back to Hub
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0">
          {activeView === "ASSIGN" && <AssignmentEngine staff={staffOptions} activeNodeIds={unavailableNodeIds} />}
          {activeView === "BOARD" && <KanbanBoard tasks={mappedTasks} />}
          {activeView === "INVENTORY" && <HardwareInventory items={mappedInventory} isLoading={isLoading} />}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 flex flex-col h-full animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      <div className="sticky -top-6 md:-top-14 z-40 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col px-6 md:px-8 pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-5 -mt-6 md:-mt-8 -mx-6 md:-mx-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase">
          Fleet Operations Hub
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider">
          Command Center for Physical Deployments
        </p>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mt-6">
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mb-1">Active Tasks</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">{activeTasksCount}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mb-1">Available Staff</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">{staffOptions.length}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mb-1">Total Hardware</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">{mappedInventory.length}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-4 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none flex flex-col">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mb-1">Pending Links</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{unlinkedBeaconsCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 flex-1 min-h-0 pb-8">
        <Card onClick={() => setActiveView("ASSIGN")} className="group cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:translate-x-1 hover: dark:hover:border-indigo-500 transition-all flex flex-col">
          <CardContent className="p-8 flex flex-col h-full justify-between gap-8">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-xl bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/50 border-2 border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-900 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Send className="h-7 w-7 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
              </div>

                <h3 className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">Assign Tasks</h3>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 mt-2">Filter maps, select unassigned nodes, and dispatch work orders to your field technicians.</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 group-hover:gap-3 transition-all gap-2">

"use client";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Loader2, RadioTower, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

export interface InventoryItem {
  id: string;
  nodeLabel: string;
  beaconId: string | null | undefined;
  rssi: number | null | undefined;
  status: "ACTIVE" | "UNLINKED";
}

export function HardwareInventory({
  items,
  isLoading,
}: {
  items: InventoryItem[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
}) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<"ALL" | "ACTIVE" | "UNLINKED">("ALL");

  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    return items.filter((item) => {
      const matchStatus = statusFilter === "ALL" || item.status === statusFilter;
      const matchSearch = !q || 
        item.nodeLabel.toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
        (item.beaconId && item.beaconId.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      return matchStatus && matchSearch;
    });
  }, [items, search, statusFilter]);

  const activeCount = items.filter((i) => i.status === "ACTIVE").length;
  const unlinkedCount = items.filter((i) => i.status === "UNLINKED").length;

  return (
    <Card className="h-full flex flex-col rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
      
      {/* Header & Controls Area */}
      <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-900 pb-6 p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl flex items-center gap-3 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100">
              <RadioTower className="h-6 w-6" />
              Hardware Databank
              {isLoading && <Loader2 className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 ml-2" />}
            </CardTitle>
            
            {/* Summary Chips */}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Badge variant="outline" className="bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-950/50 border-2 border-emerald-950 dark:border-emerald-700 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 font-black uppercase text-[10px] tracking-widest">
                Total: {items.length}
              </Badge>
              <Badge variant="outline" className="bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-800 border-2 border-emerald-950 dark:border-emerald-700 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100 px-3 py-1 font-black uppercase text-[10px] tracking-widest">
                Active: {activeCount}
              </Badge>
              <Badge variant="outline" className="bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-900 border-2 border-amber-900 dark:border-amber-600 text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-100 px-3 py-1 font-black uppercase text-[10px] tracking-widest">
                Unlinked: {unlinkedCount}
              </Badge>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <Search className="h-4 w-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-200/50" />
              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search by Node Label or Beacon ID..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border-2 border-emerald-950 dark:border-emerald-700 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 pl-10 pr-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-emerald-950/50 dark:placeholder:text-emerald-200/50 outline-none"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {(["ALL", "ACTIVE", "UNLINKED"] as const).map((opt) => (
                <button
                  key={opt}
                  onClick={() => setStatusFilter(opt)}
                  className={`px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border-2 font-black uppercase text-[10px] tracking-widest transition-colors ${
                    statusFilter === opt
                      ? "bg-emerald-950 text-white border-emerald-950 dark:bg-emerald-100 dark:text-emerald-950"
                      : "bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-100 border-emerald-950/30 dark:border-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-900/50"
                  }`}
                >
                  {opt}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="flex-1 p-0 overflow-y-auto bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950">
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col p-6 space-y-6">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((skeleton) => (
              <div key={skeleton} className="flex justify-between items-center animate-pulse border-b-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
                <div className="h-5 bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 rounded w-1/4"></div>
                <div className="h-5 bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 rounded w-20"></div>
                <div className="h-6 bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 rounded w-32"></div>
                <div className="h-5 bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 rounded w-16"></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filteredItems.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-center bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-950">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 mb-4">
              <Search className="h-8 w-8 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600" />
            </div>
            <p className="font-black text-lg text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-tight">
              No Hardware Matches
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 mt-2 max-w-sm uppercase tracking-widest leading-relaxed">
              Adjust your search filters or map new nodes in the Studio.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="min-w-[800px]">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_2fr_1fr] gap-6 px-8 py-4 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-[11px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider sticky top-0 z-10">
              <span>Node Label</span>
              <span>Status</span>
              <span>Physical Beacon ID</span>
              <span className="text-right">Calibrated RSSI</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col">
              {filteredItems.map((item, index) => {
                const isLast = index === filteredItems.length - 1;
                const isActive = item.status === "ACTIVE";
                
                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className={`grid grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_2fr_1fr] gap-6 px-8 py-5 items-center hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900/50 transition-colors ${!isLast ? "border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800" : ""}`}
                  >
                    <span className="font-black text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase">
                      {item.nodeLabel}
                    </span>
                    
                    <div>
                      <Badge 
                        variant="outline" 
                        className={`font-black text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider border-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 w-fit ${
                          isActive 
                            ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/50 dark:text-emerald-400 dark:border-emerald-800" 
                            : "bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 border-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isActive ? <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" /> : <AlertCircle className="h-3 w-3" />}
                        {item.status}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      {item.beaconId ? (
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700  dark:shadow-none px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md inline-flex w-fit uppercase">
                          {item.beaconId}
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md inline-flex w-fit uppercase">
                          PENDING LINK
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <span className={`text-lg font-black text-right ${isActive ? "text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-500" : "text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700"}`}>
                      {item.rssi ? `${item.rssi} dBm` : "---"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { LayoutDashboard, MapPin, Search, User } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

export interface Task {
  id: string;
  status: "TO_DO" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "VERIFIED";
  nodeLabel: string;
  staffName: string;
}

const STATUS_OPTIONS: Array<"ALL" | Task["status"]> = ["ALL", "TO_DO", "IN_PROGRESS", "VERIFIED"];

export function KanbanBoard({ tasks }: { tasks: Task[] }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<"ALL" | Task["status"]>("ALL");

  const columns = [
    {
      id: "TO_DO" as const,
      title: "To Do",
      bg: "bg-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-950/40",
      border: "border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-700",
      text: "text-purple-950 dark:text-purple-400",
      dot: "bg-purple-500",
    },
    {
      id: "IN_PROGRESS" as const,
      title: "In Progress",
      bg: "bg-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-950/40",
      border: "border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-700",
      text: "text-amber-950 dark:text-amber-400",
      dot: "bg-amber-500",
    },
    {
      id: "VERIFIED" as const,
      title: "Verified",
      bg: "bg-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40",
      border: "border-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-700",
      text: "text-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-400",
      dot: "bg-emerald-500",
    },
  ];

  const filteredTasks = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();

    return tasks.filter((task) => {
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === "ALL" ? true : task.status === statusFilter;
      const matchesQuery =
        !q ||
        task.nodeLabel.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        task.staffName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        task.id.toLowerCase().includes(q);

      return matchesStatus && matchesQuery;
    });
  }, [tasks, query, statusFilter]);

  return (
    <Card className="h-full min-h-0 flex flex-col rounded-3xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
      <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-amber-300 dark:bg-amber-950/40 p-5 md:p-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">
          <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <CardTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-xl flex items-center gap-3 text-zinc-950 dark:text-amber-400">
              <LayoutDashboard className="h-6 w-6" />
              Task Control Board
            </CardTitle>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {STATUS_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                const active = statusFilter === opt;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt}
                    onClick={() => setStatusFilter(opt)}
                    className={`rounded-full border-2 px-5 py-2.5 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] font-black transition-all ${
                      active
                        ? "border-zinc-950 bg-zinc-950 text-white  dark:border-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 "
                        : "border-zinc-950/20 bg-white/50 text-zinc-700 hover:border-zinc-950 hover:bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/40 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:border-zinc-600 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {opt === "ALL" ? "All Tracks" : opt.replace("_", " ")}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto]">
            <div className="relative max-w-2xl">
              <Search className="h-5 w-5 absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-zinc-500" />
              <input
                value={query}
                onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search specific nodes, staff members, or task IDs..."
                className="w-full rounded-2xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 pl-12 pr-4 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-500 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-600 outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 dark:focus:border-zinc-600 transition-colors"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-400 rounded-2xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 px-6 py-4">
              Showing {filteredTasks.length} of {tasks.length} Active
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="flex-1 min-h-0 p-5 md:p-8 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-x-auto">
        <div className="grid gap-6 grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 min-w-225 h-full min-h-0">
          {columns.map((col) => {
            const columnTasks = filteredTasks.filter((t) => t.status === col.id);

            return (
              <div
                key={col.id}
                className={`flex flex-col rounded-3xl border-2 ${col.border} overflow-hidden bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 h-full  dark:shadow-none`}
              >
                <div className={`p-5 border-b-2 ${col.border} flex items-center justify-between ${col.bg}`}>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-current ${col.dot}`} />
                    <h3 className={`font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm ${col.text}`}>
                      {col.title}
                    </h3>
                  </div>
                  <Badge
                    variant="outline"
                    className={`border-2 font-black text-xs rounded-full px-3 py-1 ${col.border} ${col.text} bg-white/60 dark:bg-zinc-950/60`}
                  >
                    {columnTasks.length}
                  </Badge>
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 p-5 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 custom-scrollbar">
                  {columnTasks.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-950 p-8 text-center text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500">
                      Queue Empty
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    columnTasks.map((task) => (
                      <div
                        key={task.id}
                        className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900/80 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-all border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-5 hover:-translate-y-1 hover: dark:hover:shadow-none cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-4">
                          <MapPin className="h-5 w-5 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-100 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                          <div className="font-black text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 uppercase leading-tight">
                            {task.nodeLabel}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest border-t-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pt-3">
                          <User className="h-4 w-4" />
                          {task.staffName}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

      suppressHydrationWarning

"use client";

import { authActions } from "@/lib/actions/auth";
// FIXED: Import the strictly typed DTO from our centralized types file
import { RegisterOrgRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";
import * as React from "react";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

export interface User {
  id: string;
  email?: string;
  role?: string;
  user_metadata?: {
    organization_id?: string;
    display_name?: string;
  };
}

interface AuthState {
  isLoggedIn: boolean;
  user: User | null;
  activeOrgId: string | null;
}

interface AuthContextType extends AuthState {
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  // FIXED: Updated payload type
  register: (payload: RegisterOrgRequest) => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = React.createContext<AuthContextType | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [authState, setAuthState] = useState<AuthState>({
    isLoggedIn: false,
    user: null,
    activeOrgId: null,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    // Hydrate the UI state entirely from localStorage
    const storedUser = localStorage.getItem("echonav_user");
    if (storedUser) {
      const parsedUser = JSON.parse(storedUser);
      setAuthState({
        isLoggedIn: true,
        user: parsedUser,
        activeOrgId: parsedUser.user_metadata?.organization_id || null,
      });
    }
  }, []);

  const login = useCallback(async (email: string, password: string) => {
    const response = await authActions.login(email, password);
    
    const { token, role, organisationId, userId } = response;

    const userData: User = {
      id: String(userId),
      email: email,
      role: role,
      user_metadata: {
        organization_id: String(organisationId),
        display_name: email.split("@")[0],
      },
    };

    // 1. Store the UI user state
    localStorage.setItem("echonav_user", JSON.stringify(userData));
    
    // 2. THE FIX: Store the REAL JWT in a standard cookie so both Client and Server can read it
    document.cookie = `echonav_token=${token}; path=/; max-age=86400; SameSite=Lax`;

    setAuthState({
      isLoggedIn: true,
      user: userData,
      activeOrgId: String(organisationId),
    });

    window.location.href = "/dashboard";
  }, []);

  // FIXED: Updated parameter type
  const register = useCallback(async (payload: RegisterOrgRequest) => {
    await authActions.register(payload);
    // The backend login requires a password, which is optional on the frontend type but required here
    if (payload.password) {
      await login(payload.email, payload.password);
    } else {
      // Fallback if password isn't passed through for some reason
      window.location.href = "/auth?tab=login";
    }
  }, [login]);

  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      await authActions.logout();
    } catch {
      console.warn("Cleared local state regardless of server response.");
    } finally {
      // 1. Clear Local Storage
      localStorage.removeItem("echonav_user");
      
      // 2. THE FIX: Destroy the real JWT cookie
      document.cookie = `echonav_token=; path=/; expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT`;

      setAuthState({ isLoggedIn: false, user: null, activeOrgId: null });
      window.location.href = "/";
    }
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      ...authState,
      login,
      logout,
      register,
    }),
    [authState, login, logout, register]
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = React.useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider");
  return context;
}
