"use client";

import { Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { ModeToggle } from "@/components/layout/theme-toggle";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { SidebarTrigger } from "@/components/ui/sidebar";
import { useIsMobile } from "@/hooks/use-mobile";

import { NotificationBell } from "@/components/layout/NotificationBell";
import { UserAccountNav } from "./UserAccountNav";

export function Header() {
  const { isLoggedIn } = useAuth();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
  const [isMobileMenuOpen, setIsMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const closeMenu = () => setIsMobileMenuOpen(false);

  const isPublicRoute =
    pathname === "/" ||
    pathname.startsWith("/auth") ||
    pathname.startsWith("/reset-password");
  const showDashboardHeader = isLoggedIn && !isPublicRoute;

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 w-full shrink-0 border-b border-white/10 bg-transparent backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 items-center justify-between px-4 md:px-8">
        {/* =========================================
            LOGGED IN VIEW (Dashboard Style)
            ========================================= */}
        {showDashboardHeader ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              {isMobile && <SidebarTrigger className="md:hidden" />}

              <Image
                src="./EchonavLogo.svg"
                alt="EchoNav Logo"
                width={30}
                height={20}
                className="mr-2 dark:invert"
              />
              <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">EchoNav</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 sm:gap-5">
              <NotificationBell />
              <ModeToggle />
              <div className="h-6 w-0.5 bg-border mx-1 hidden sm:block"></div>
              <UserAccountNav />
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* =========================================
             LOGGED OUT VIEW (Landing Page Style)
             ========================================= */
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-8">
              <Link href="/" className="flex items-center" onClick={closeMenu}>
                <Image
                  src="EchonavLogo.svg"
                  alt="EchoNav Logo"
                  width={30}
                  height={20}
                  className="mr-2 dark:invert"
                />
                <span className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                  EchoNav
                </span>
              </Link>

              <nav className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-6">
                {["Services", "Solutions", "About", "Contact"].map((item) => (
                  <Link
                    key={item}
                    href={`#`}
                    className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                  >
                    {item}
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </nav>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2">
                <Link href="/auth?tab=login">
                  <Button variant="outline">Log in</Button>
                </Link>
                <Link href="/auth?tab=signup">
                  <Button>Sign up</Button>
                </Link>
              </div>

              <ModeToggle />

              {isMobile && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setIsMobileMenuOpen(!isMobileMenuOpen)}
                  className="md:hidden flex items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-md hover:bg-accent transition-colors"
                  aria-label="Toggle mobile menu"
                >
                  {isMobileMenuOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* =========================================
          MOBILE DROPDOWN MENU (Logged out only)
          ========================================= */}
      {isMobileMenuOpen && isMobile && !showDashboardHeader && (
        <div className="md:hidden border-t bg-background px-6 py-6 shadow-lg">
          <div className="flex flex-col space-y-5">
            <nav className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
              {["Services", "Solutions", "About", "Contact"].map((item) => (
                <Link
                  key={item}
                  href={`/${item.toLowerCase()}`}
                  onClick={closeMenu}
                  className="text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground transition-colors"
                >
                  {item}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </nav>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-4 border-t">
              <Link href="/auth?tab=login" onClick={closeMenu}>
                <Button variant="outline" className="w-full">
                  Log in
                </Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/auth?tab=signup" onClick={closeMenu}>
                <Button className="w-full">Sign up</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { Bell, Info, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

// Mock data for UI review
const mockNotifications = [
  {
    id: "1",
    type: "error",
    title: "Hardware Failure",
    message: "Beacon UUID-8X92 has gone offline.",
  },
  {
    id: "2",
    type: "warning",
    title: "Calibration Pending",
    message: "Task assigned to Michael Chen is overdue.",
  },
  {
    id: "3",
    type: "info",
    title: "New Map Vectorized",
    message: "Science Block Floor 2 is ready.",
  },
];

export function NotificationBell() {
  const [isDesktopOpen, setIsDesktopOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isMobileOpen, setIsMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  const notifications = mockNotifications;

  const getIcon = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "warning":
        return (
          <TriangleAlert className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-500" />
        );
      case "error":
        return (
          <TriangleAlert className="h-5 w-5 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500" />
        );
      default:
        return (
          <Info className="h-5 w-5 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400" />
        );
    }
  };

  const notificationList = (
    <div className="flex flex-col max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950">
      {notifications.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="p-8 text-center space-y-2">
          <Bell className="h-8 w-8 mx-auto text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700" />
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500">
            You&apos;re all caught up!
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        notifications.map((notification, index) => {
          const isLast = index === notifications.length - 1;
          return (
            <div
              key={notification.id}
              className={`p-4 flex gap-4 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900/50 ${!isLast ? "border-b-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800" : ""}`}
            >
              <div
                className={`mt-0.5 w-8 h-8 shrink-0 rounded-lg border-2 flex items-center justify-center ${notification.type === "error" ? "bg-red-50 border-red-200 dark:bg-red-950/30 dark:border-red-900" : notification.type === "warning" ? "bg-amber-50 border-amber-200 dark:bg-amber-950/30 dark:border-amber-900" : "bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-200 dark:bg-indigo-950/30 dark:border-indigo-900"}`}
              >
                {getIcon(notification.type)}
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 space-y-1 min-w-0">
                <p className="text-sm font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 truncate">
                  {notification.title}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 leading-relaxed line-clamp-2">
                  {notification.message}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })
      )}
    </div>
  );

  // THE FIX: Changed to rounded-xl for a softer box, removed the outer span wrapper
  const triggerButton = (
    <Button
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon"
      className="relative h-10 w-10 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white transition-all dark:shadow-none active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none shrink-0"
    >
      <Bell className="h-5 w-5" />

      {notifications.length > 0 && (
        <span className="absolute -top-1.5 -right-1.5 flex h-4 w-4">
          <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-red-400 opacity-75"></span>
          <span className="relative inline-flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-500 text-[9px] font-black text-white border-2 border-white dark:border-zinc-950 shadow-sm">
            {notifications.length}
          </span>
        </span>
      )}
    </Button>
  );

  return (
    <>
      {/* --- DESKTOP VIEW (Popover) --- */}
      <div className="hidden md:block">
        <Popover open={isDesktopOpen} onOpenChange={setIsDesktopOpen}>
          <PopoverTrigger asChild>{triggerButton}</PopoverTrigger>
          {/* THE FIX: Changed to rounded-2xl for maximum softness on the tray */}
          <PopoverContent
            className="w-80 p-0 rounded-2xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:shadow-none overflow-hidden mt-2"
            align="end"
          >
            <div className="px-4 py-3 font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
              Notifications
            </div>
            {notificationList}
          </PopoverContent>
        </Popover>
      </div>

      {/* --- MOBILE VIEW (Dialog) --- */}
      <div className="md:hidden">
        <Dialog open={isMobileOpen} onOpenChange={setIsMobileOpen}>
          <DialogTrigger asChild>{triggerButton}</DialogTrigger>
          <DialogContent className="w-[90%] rounded-2xl p-0 gap-0 overflow-hidden border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950">
            <DialogHeader className="px-4 py-4 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-left">
              <DialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                Notifications
              </DialogTitle>
            </DialogHeader>
            {notificationList}
            <div className="p-3 border-t-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900">
              <Button
                variant="ghost"
                className="w-full font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:shadow-none hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-xl"
                onClick={() => setIsMobileOpen(false)}
              >
                Close Menu
              </Button>
            </div>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";

export function QueryProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  // We use useState to ensure the QueryClient is only initialized once per session,
  // preventing data loss during React component re-renders.
  const [queryClient] = useState(
    () =>
      new QueryClient({
        defaultOptions: {
          queries: {
            staleTime: 60 * 1000, // Data remains fresh for 1 minute
            refetchOnWindowFocus: false, // Prevents spamming the backend when switching tabs
            retry: 1, // Only retry failed requests once
          },
        },
      })
  );

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      {children}
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  IdCard,
  LayoutDashboard,
  Map,
  RadioTower,
  Settings,
  Users
} from "lucide-react";
import Image from "next/image";
import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import { useState } from "react";

const navLinks = [
  { href: "/dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { href: "/map-studio", label: "Map Studio", icon: Map },
  { href: "/fleet", label: "Fleet Management", icon: RadioTower },
  { href: "/staff", label: "Staff Management", icon: IdCard },
  { href: "/user-management", label: "User Management", icon: Users },
];

export function Sidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [manualCollapsed, setManualCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const { isLoggedIn } = useAuth();

  // THE FIX: Completely hide the sidebar if we are on an auth/activation page
  const isPublicRoute = pathname === "/" || pathname.startsWith("/auth") || pathname.startsWith("/reset-password");
  
  if (!isLoggedIn || isPublicRoute) return null;

  const isAutoCollapsed = /\/map-studio\/[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/.test(pathname);
  const isCollapsed = manualCollapsed || isAutoCollapsed;

  // Define if the settings route is active
  const isSettingsActive = pathname.startsWith("/settings");

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "relative flex flex-col h-screen transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out shrink-0 bg-background border-r border-chart-3",
        isCollapsed ? "w-20" : "w-64",
      )}
    >
      {/* HEADER SECTION */}
      <div
        className={cn(
          "h-20 flex items-center border-b border-chart-3 shrink-0 overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300",
          isCollapsed ? "justify-center px-0" : "justify-between px-6",
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="bg-black dark:bg-white p-1.5 rounded-lg shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Image
              src="/EchoDash.svg"
              alt="EchoDash Logo"
              width={24}
              height={24}
              className="invert dark:invert-0"
              priority
            />
          </div>
          {!isCollapsed && (
            <span className="font-bold text-xl tracking-tight whitespace-nowrap">
              EchoDash
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        {!isCollapsed && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div className="h-6 w-px bg-chart-3 mx-1" />
            <Button
              variant="default"
              size="icon"
              onClick={() => setManualCollapsed(true)}
              className="group h-8 w-8 rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-chart-2 dark:hover:bg-chart-2"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4 transition-colors text-white dark:text-black group-hover:text-black dark:group-hover:text-black" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* COLLAPSED TOGGLE VIEW */}
      {isCollapsed && (
        <div className="flex justify-center py-4 border-b border-chart-3 shrink-0">
          <Button
            variant="default"
            size="icon"
            onClick={() => setManualCollapsed(false)}
            className="group h-8 w-8 rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-chart-2 dark:hover:bg-chart-2"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 transition-colors text-white dark:text-black group-hover:text-white dark:group-hover:text-black" />
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* NAVIGATION SECTION */}
      <nav
        className={cn(
          "flex-1 py-6 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden transition-all duration-300",
          isCollapsed ? "px-2" : "px-4",
        )}

        {navLinks.map((link) => {
          const isActive =
            link.href === "/map-studio"
              ? pathname === "/map-studio"
              : pathname.startsWith(link.href);

          return (
            <Link
              key={link.href}
              href={link.href}
              title={isCollapsed ? link.label : undefined}
              className={cn(
                "group flex items-center rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 font-medium text-sm border",
                isCollapsed
                  ? "justify-center h-12 w-12 mx-auto gap-0"
                  : "gap-3 py-3 px-4",
                isActive
                  ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary shadow-md shadow-primary/20 scale-[1.02]"
                  : "bg-transparent text-muted-foreground border-transparent hover:bg-primary hover:text-secondary hover:border-border/50",
              )}
            >
              <link.icon
                className={cn(
                  "w-4.5 h-4.5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:scale-110",
                  isActive ? "" : "opacity-70 group-hover:opacity-100",
                )}
              />
              {!isCollapsed && <span className="truncate">{link.label}</span>}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* FOOTER SECTION */}
      <div
        className={cn(
          "border-t border-chart-3 bg-sidebar/50 shrink-0 transition-all duration-300",
          isCollapsed ? "p-2" : "p-4",
        )}
      >
        <Link
          href="/settings"
          title={isCollapsed ? "Settings" : undefined}
          className={cn(
            "group flex items-center rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 font-medium text-sm border",
            isCollapsed
              ? "justify-center h-12 w-12 mx-auto"
              : "gap-3 py-3 px-4 w-full",
            isSettingsActive
              ? "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border-primary shadow-md shadow-primary/20 scale-[1.02]"
              : "bg-transparent text-muted-foreground border-transparent hover:bg-primary hover:text-secondary hover:border-border/50",

"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { Moon, Sun } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function ModeToggle() {
  const { resolvedTheme, setTheme } = useTheme();

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    // Flips the theme to the opposite of whatever is currently active
    setTheme(resolvedTheme === "dark" ? "light" : "dark");
  };

  return (
    <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={toggleTheme}>
      <Sun className="h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-0 scale-100 transition-all dark:-rotate-90 dark:scale-0" />
      <Moon className="absolute h-[1.2rem] w-[1.2rem] rotate-90 scale-0 transition-all dark:rotate-0 dark:scale-100" />
      <span className="sr-only">Toggle theme</span>
    </Button>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Avatar, AvatarFallback, AvatarImage } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { LogOut, Settings, User as UserIcon } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export function UserAccountNav() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [isMounted, setIsMounted] = useState(false);

  // Fixes the Hydration Mismatch AND satisfies the linter by making it asynchronous
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => setIsMounted(true), 0);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, []);

  // Show a blank brutalist box for a split second while loading
  if (!isMounted) {
    return (
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        className="relative h-10 w-10 rounded-lg border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden p-0"
      >
        <Avatar className="h-full w-full rounded-none">
          <AvatarFallback className="bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900"></AvatarFallback>
        </Avatar>
      </Button>
    );
  }

  const displayName = user?.user_metadata?.display_name || "Admin";
  const userEmail = user?.email || "No email provided";

  // Extract initials safely
  const initials = displayName
    .split(" ")
    .map((n: string) => n[0])
    .join("")
    .substring(0, 2)
    .toUpperCase();

  const handleSignOut = async () => {
    try {
      await logout();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Error signing out:", error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
        {/* Brutalist Avatar Button */}
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          className="relative h-10 w-10 rounded-lg border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-all overflow-hidden shrink-0 p-0 dark:shadow-none active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
        >
          <Avatar className="h-full w-full rounded-none">
            <AvatarImage src="" alt={displayName} />
            <AvatarFallback className="bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-950 dark:text-white font-black text-xs">
              {initials}
            </AvatarFallback>
          </Avatar>
        </Button>
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>

      {/* Brutalist Dropdown Content */}
      <DropdownMenuContent
        className="w-64 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl dark:shadow-none p-2 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950"
        align="end"
        forceMount
      >
        <DropdownMenuLabel className="font-normal p-2">
          <div className="flex flex-col space-y-1">
            <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider leading-none text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
              {displayName}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold leading-none text-zinc-500">
              {userEmail}
            </p>
          </div>
        </DropdownMenuLabel>

        <DropdownMenuSeparator className="bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 my-1" />

        <DropdownMenuItem
          asChild
          className="cursor-pointer font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 focus:bg-zinc-100 dark:focus:bg-zinc-800 p-3 transition-colors"
        >
          <Link href="/settings">
            <Settings className="mr-3 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500" />
            <span>System Settings</span>
          </Link>
        </DropdownMenuItem>

        <DropdownMenuItem
          asChild
          className="cursor-pointer font-bold rounded-lg hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 focus:bg-zinc-100 dark:focus:bg-zinc-800 p-3 transition-colors"
        >
          <Link href="#">
            <UserIcon className="mr-3 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-500" />
            <span>My Profile</span>
          </Link>
        </DropdownMenuItem>

        <DropdownMenuSeparator className="bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 my-1" />

        <DropdownMenuItem
          className="text-red-600 focus:text-red-600 focus:bg-red-50 dark:text-red-500 dark:focus:bg-red-950/30 cursor-pointer font-bold rounded-lg p-3 transition-colors"
          onSelect={(e) => {
            // Prevent default to stop the dropdown from destroying itself before the window navigates
            e.preventDefault();
            handleSignOut();
          }}
        >
          <LogOut className="mr-3 h-4 w-4" />
          <span>Sign Out</span>
        </DropdownMenuItem>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardFooter } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Activity, AlertTriangle, ArrowRight, Loader2, Map, MapPin, Shield, ShieldAlert, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import Link from "next/link";
import React, { useState } from "react";
// THE FIX: Combined both hooks from alphas and Ticket-8
import { useUpdateBuildingPrivacy, useGetBuildingStats } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";

// ============================================================================
// 1. ISOLATED PRIVACY TOGGLE COMPONENT (From alphas)
// ============================================================================

interface PrivacyToggleProps {
  buildingId: string;
  buildingName: string;
  currentPrivacy: "PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE";
  orgId: number;
}

export function PrivacyToggle({ buildingId, buildingName, currentPrivacy, orgId }: PrivacyToggleProps) {
  const [showDialog, setShowDialog] = useState(false);
  const { mutate, isPending } = useUpdateBuildingPrivacy();

  const targetPrivacy = currentPrivacy === "PUBLIC" ? "PRIVATE" : "PUBLIC";

  const handleConfirm = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault(); 
    
    mutate(
      { buildingId: Number(buildingId), orgId, data: { privacyLevel: targetPrivacy } },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setShowDialog(false);
        },
        onError: (error) => {
          alert(`Failed to update privacy: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown error"}`);
          setShowDialog(false); 
        }
      }
    );
  };

  const confirmButtonColor = targetPrivacy === "PUBLIC" 
    ? "bg-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-zinc-950" 
    : "bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-zinc-950";

  return (
    <>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <span className={`text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase ${currentPrivacy === "PUBLIC" ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-amber-600"}`}>
          {currentPrivacy}
        </span>
        <Switch
          checked={currentPrivacy === "PUBLIC"}
          disabled={isPending}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            setShowDialog(true);
          }}
          className="data-[state=checked]:bg-emerald-500 data-[state=unchecked]:bg-amber-500 border-2 border-zinc-950 scale-75 m-0 cursor-pointer"
        />
      </div>

      <AlertDialog open={showDialog} onOpenChange={setShowDialog}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl">
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-tight text-xl">Confirm Privacy Change</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-500">
              Are you sure you want to change the privacy level of <span className="text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">{buildingName}</span> to {targetPrivacy}?
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter className="gap-3 sm:gap-4 mt-2">
            <AlertDialogCancel 
              disabled={isPending} 
              className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-11 px-6  dark:shadow-none hover:bg-zinc-100 transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none"
            >
              Cancel
            </AlertDialogCancel>
            
            <Button
              disabled={isPending}
              onClick={handleConfirm}
              className={`border-2 border-zinc-950 font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-11 px-6  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none ${confirmButtonColor}`}
            >
              {isPending ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                  Updating...
                </>
              ) : (
                "Confirm Change"
              )}
            </Button>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </>
  );
}

        {/* Confirm Delete Popup Wrapper */}

                Delete Building

"bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 hover:bg-amber-200" : ""}`}>
          {building.privacy === "PRIVATE" ? <ShieldAlert className="w-3 h-3 mr-1.5" /> : <Shield className="w-3 h-3 mr-1.5" />}
          {building.privacy}

"use client";

import { ArrowRight, Layers, Map, Ruler, Trash2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react"; // <-- Added AlertCircle
import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";

import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";

import { queryKeys } from "@/hooks/api/queryKeys";
import { useDeleteFloorPlan, useGetFloorPlans, useGetFloorPlanStats } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio"; // <-- Added useGetFloorPlanStats
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { CreateFloorPlan } from "./CreateFloorPlan";

const FLOORS_PER_PAGE = 6;

export interface FloorData {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  nodes: number;
  edges: number;
  status: "Active" | "Processing" | "Error";
  scale: number | null; 
}

export function BuildingMapsComposer({ buildingId }: { buildingId: string }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const buildingIdNum = Number(buildingId);
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  
  // NEW: Alert State for Errors
  const [alertMessage, setAlertMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { data: floorPlansDto, isLoading, isError } = useGetFloorPlans(buildingIdNum);
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteFloorPlan();

  const mappedFloors: FloorData[] = (floorPlansDto || []).map((fp) => ({
    id: String(fp.id || ""),
    name: fp.name || "Unnamed Floor",
    nodes: 0, 
    edges: 0,
    status: "Active",
    scale: fp.scale ?? null,
  }));

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(mappedFloors.length / FLOORS_PER_PAGE));
  const safeCurrentPage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);
  const startIndex = (safeCurrentPage - 1) * FLOORS_PER_PAGE;
  const paginatedFloors = mappedFloors.slice(startIndex, startIndex + FLOORS_PER_PAGE);

  const handleUploadSuccess = () => {
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.mapStudio.floorPlans(buildingIdNum) });
    setCurrentPage(1); 
  };

  const handleDeleteFloor = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteMutation.mutateAsync({ floorPlanId: Number(id) });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) setAlertMessage(error.message); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="sticky -top-6 md:-top-14 z-40 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 -mt-6 md:-mt-8 -mx-6 md:-mx-8 px-6 md:px-8 pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-5 mb-8">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <Map className="h-5 w-5 text-zinc-500" />
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase">
              Building Directory
            </h1>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Manage individual floor plans and maps for location #{buildingId}.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <CreateFloorPlan buildingId={buildingIdNum} onUploadSuccess={handleUploadSuccess} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 pb-4">
          {[1, 2].map((skeleton) => (
            <div key={skeleton} className="h-32 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {isError && (
        <div className="p-6 text-center border-2 border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/20 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sm">
          Failed to load floor plans. Please check your connection.
        </div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && mappedFloors.length === 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 rounded-xl bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 mt-8">
          <div className="h-16 w-16 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-4  dark:shadow-none">
            <Layers className="h-8 w-8 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-tight">No Floors Found</h3>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 mt-2 max-w-md">This building does not have any architectural maps registered yet.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && paginatedFloors.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 pb-4">
          {/* <-- Replaced static cards with our newly extracted FloorPlanCard --> */}
          {paginatedFloors.map((floor) => (
            <FloorPlanCard 
              key={floor.id} 
              buildingId={buildingId} 
              floor={floor} 
              onDelete={handleDeleteFloor} 
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* FINAL SHADCN ALERT DIALOG */}
      <AlertDialog open={!!alertMessage} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setAlertMessage(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-6 sm:max-w-[425px]">
          
          <AlertDialogHeader className="flex flex-row items-center gap-4 space-y-0">
            <div className="bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg border-2 border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 shrink-0">
              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </div>
            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 m-0">
              System Alert
            </AlertDialogTitle>
          </AlertDialogHeader>

          <div className="py-2">
            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">
              <span className="text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200">{alertMessage}</span>
              {"\n\nPlease verify your connection."}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </div>

          <AlertDialogFooter className="mt-2">
            <AlertDialogAction 
              onClick={() => setAlertMessage(null)}
              className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white font-black uppercase text-sm tracking-wider h-11 px-8 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              Acknowledge
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
          
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

    </div>
  );
}

// --- TICKET 8 EXTRACTED COMPONENT ---
interface FloorPlanCardProps {
  buildingId: string;
  floor: FloorData;
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
}

function FloorPlanCard({ buildingId, floor, onDelete }: FloorPlanCardProps) {
  // <-- Safe hook call inside isolated component
  const { data: stats } = useGetFloorPlanStats(Number(floor.id));

  return (
    <Card className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 group">
      <CardContent className="p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="h-12 w-12 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <Layers className="h-6 w-6 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-black text-lg text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-tight">{floor.name}</h3>
            {/* <-- Dynamic UI mapped to backend counts --> */}
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest mt-1">
              {stats?.nodeCount || 0} Nodes | {stats?.edgeCount || 0} Edges
            </p>
            <div className={`mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border text-[9px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider ${floor.scale ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700' : 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700'}`}>
              <Ruler className="h-3 w-3" />
              {floor.scale ? `Scale: ${floor.scale.toFixed(4)}` : "Uncalibrated Scale"}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <AlertDialog>
            <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
              <Button variant="outline" className="h-10 w-10 p-0 border-2 border-zinc-950 text-zinc-500 hover:text-red-600 ">
                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            </AlertDialogTrigger>
            <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 rounded-xl">
              <AlertDialogHeader>
                <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase">Remove Floor Plan?</AlertDialogTitle>
                <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold">This cannot be undone.</AlertDialogDescription>
              </AlertDialogHeader>
              <AlertDialogFooter>
                <AlertDialogCancel className="border-2 border-zinc-950">Cancel</AlertDialogCancel>
                <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => onDelete(floor.id)} className="bg-red-600 border-2 border-zinc-950">Delete</AlertDialogAction>
              </AlertDialogFooter>
            </AlertDialogContent>
          </AlertDialog>

          <Button asChild variant="outline" className="border-2 border-zinc-950 font-bold uppercase text-[11px] h-10 ">
            <Link href={`/map-studio/${buildingId}/${floor.id}`}>Edit Canvas <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 ml-2" /></Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { useGooglePlacesAutocomplete } from "@/hooks/AutoAddressGoogleMaps";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { BuildingData } from "./BuildingCard";

interface CreateBuildingProps {
  onAdd: (building: Omit<BuildingData, "id" | "stats" | "health" | "updatedAt">) => void;
}

function BuildingForm({ onAdd, onClose }: { onAdd: (building: Omit<BuildingData, "id" | "stats" | "health" | "updatedAt">) => void; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [privacy, setPrivacy] = useState<"PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE">("PUBLIC");
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [street, setStreet] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [stateProv, setStateProv] = useState("");
  const [zip, setZip] = useState("");

  const streetInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Robust Google Autocomplete
  useGooglePlacesAutocomplete(streetInputRef, "", (details) => {
    setStreet(details.streetAddress);
    setCity(details.city);
    setStateProv(details.stateProvince);
    setZip(details.postalCode);
  });

  const handleRegister = () => {
    if (!name || !street) return;

    // Construct the display address for the card
    const fullAddress = `${street}${city ? `, ${city}` : ""}${zip ? `, ${zip}` : ""}`;
    
    onAdd({
      name,
      address: fullAddress,
      privacy,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <>
      <div className="grid gap-6 p-6">
        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Building Name
          </Label>
          <Input
            value={name}
            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g., Science Block"
            className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Street Address
          </Label>
          <Input
            ref={streetInputRef}
            value={street}
            onChange={(e) => setStreet(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search address..."
            className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
            autoComplete="off"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">City</Label>
            <Input
              value={city}
              onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
              className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">State</Label>
            <Input
              value={stateProv}
              onChange={(e) => setStateProv(e.target.value)}
              className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">Zip</Label>
            <Input
              value={zip}
              onChange={(e) => setZip(e.target.value)}
              className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-2">
          <Label className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Privacy Level
          </Label>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  w-fit">
            <span className={`text-[11px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase ${privacy === "PUBLIC" ? "text-emerald-600" : "text-amber-600"}`}>
              {privacy}
            </span>
            <Switch
              checked={privacy === "PUBLIC"}
              onCheckedChange={(c) => setPrivacy(c ? "PUBLIC" : "PRIVATE")}
              className="data-[state=checked]:bg-emerald-500 data-[state=unchecked]:bg-amber-500 border-2 border-zinc-950 m-0"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <DialogFooter className="p-6 pt-0 border-t-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 mt-auto flex-row justify-end gap-3 sm:gap-0">
        <Button 
          type="button"
          onClick={handleRegister} 
          className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10  transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none"
        >
          Register Building
        </Button>
      </DialogFooter>
    </>
  );
}

export function CreateBuilding({ onAdd }: CreateBuildingProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-bold h-11 px-6  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none shrink-0 uppercase tracking-wider text-xs">
          <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          ADD NEW BUILDING
        </Button>
      </DialogTrigger>

      <DialogContent
        onFocusOutside={(e) => { if (e.target instanceof Element && e.target.closest(".pac-container")) e.preventDefault(); }}
        onInteractOutside={(e) => { if (e.target instanceof Element && e.target.closest(".pac-container")) e.preventDefault(); }}
        onPointerDownOutside={(e) => { if (e.target instanceof Element && e.target.closest(".pac-container")) e.preventDefault(); }}
        className="sm:max-w-125 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-0 overflow-hidden"
      >
        <DialogHeader className="bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-6 pb-5">
          <DialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-lg">Add New Building</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Register a physical building profile.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        
        <BuildingForm onAdd={onAdd} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { mapStudioActions } from "@/lib/actions/mapStudio";
import { FileText, Plus, UploadCloud, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react"; // <-- Added AlertCircle
import { useRef, useState } from "react";

// SHADCN IMPORTS
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";

interface CreateFloorPlanProps {
  buildingId: number;
  onUploadSuccess: () => void;
}

export function CreateFloorPlan({ buildingId, onUploadSuccess }: CreateFloorPlanProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  
  // NEW: Alert State
  const [alertMessage, setAlertMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [newFloorLabel, setNewFloorLabel] = useState("");
  const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileSelect = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files.length > 0) {
      setSelectedFile(e.target.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleUpload = async () => {
    if (!newFloorLabel.trim() || !selectedFile) {
      setAlertMessage("Please provide both a label and a file."); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
      return;
    }

    setIsUploading(true);
    try {
      const res = await mapStudioActions.uploadFloorPlan(buildingId, newFloorLabel, selectedFile);
      
      // If we just uploaded an .echonav file, catch the transient nodes before switching screens
      if (res.transientNodes || res.transientEdges) {
        sessionStorage.setItem(`echonav_import_${res.id}`, JSON.stringify({
          nodes: res.transientNodes || [],
          edges: res.transientEdges || []
        }));
      }
      
      onUploadSuccess();
      setOpen(false);
      setNewFloorLabel("");
      setSelectedFile(null);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) setAlertMessage(error.message); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
    } finally {
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(isOpen) => {
        setOpen(isOpen);
        if (!isOpen) { setNewFloorLabel(""); setSelectedFile(null); }
      }}>
        <DialogTrigger asChild>
          <Button className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-bold h-11 px-6  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none shrink-0 uppercase tracking-wider text-xs">
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            ADD NEW MAP
          </Button>
        </DialogTrigger>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-125 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-0 overflow-hidden">
          <DialogHeader className="bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 p-6 pb-5">
            <DialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-lg text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
              Add Floor Plan
            </DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-500 text-xs mt-1">
              Upload a structural map specific to this building.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>

        <div className="grid gap-6 p-6">
          <div className="grid gap-2">
            <Label htmlFor="floor-label" className="font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
              Floor Label
            </Label>
            <Input
              id="floor-label"
              value={newFloorLabel}
              onChange={(e) => setNewFloorLabel(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g., Ground Floor"
              className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none focus-visible:ring-0 font-medium"
            />
          </div>

          <div 
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${selectedFile ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/20' : 'border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'}`}
          >
            <input 
              type="file" 
              ref={fileInputRef} 
              className="hidden" 
              accept=".dwg,.svg,.pdf,.png,.echonav"
              onChange={handleFileSelect}
            />
            <div className={`h-12 w-12 rounded-lg border-2 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 transition-transform  dark:shadow-none ${selectedFile ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500 text-white' : 'border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 group-hover:scale-110'}`}>
              {selectedFile ? <FileText className="h-6 w-6" /> : <UploadCloud className="h-6 w-6" />}
            </div>

            <div 
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center transition-colors cursor-pointer group ${selectedFile ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/20' : 'border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'}`}
            >
              <input 
                type="file" 
                ref={fileInputRef} 
                className="hidden" 
                accept=".dwg,.svg,.pdf,.png"
                onChange={handleFileSelect}
              />
              <div className={`h-12 w-12 rounded-lg border-2 flex items-center justify-center mb-4 transition-transform  dark:shadow-none ${selectedFile ? 'border-indigo-500 bg-indigo-500 text-white' : 'border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 group-hover:scale-110'}`}>
                {selectedFile ? <FileText className="h-6 w-6" /> : <UploadCloud className="h-6 w-6" />}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
                {selectedFile ? 'File Selected' : 'Upload or drag and drop'}
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold mt-1 text-zinc-500">
                {selectedFile ? selectedFile.name : '.DWG, .SVG, .PDF, or High-Res .PNG'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          </div>

          <DialogFooter className="p-6 pt-0 border-t-2 border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 mt-auto flex-row justify-end gap-3 sm:gap-0">
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 font-bold uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10  dark:shadow-none">
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button 
              onClick={handleUpload} 
              disabled={isUploading || !newFloorLabel.trim() || !selectedFile}
              className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isUploading ? 'Uploading...' : 'Upload Floor Plan'}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* FINAL SHADCN ALERT DIALOG */}
      <AlertDialog open={!!alertMessage} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setAlertMessage(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-6 sm:max-w-[425px]">
          
          <AlertDialogHeader className="flex flex-row items-center gap-4 space-y-0">
            <div className="bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg border-2 border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 shrink-0">
              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </div>
            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 m-0">
              System Alert
            </AlertDialogTitle>
          </AlertDialogHeader>

          <div className="py-2">
            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">
              <span className="text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200">{alertMessage}</span>
              {"\n\nPlease verify your connection."}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </div>

          <AlertDialogFooter className="mt-2">
            <AlertDialogAction 
              onClick={() => setAlertMessage(null)}
              className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white font-black uppercase text-sm tracking-wider h-11 px-8 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              Acknowledge
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
          
        </AlertDialogContent>

    </>
  );

"use client";

import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/progress";
import { useGetSpatialGraph, useUpdateFloorPlan, useUpdateSpatialGraph } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";
import { useCalculateRoute } from "@/hooks/api/useNavigation";
import { UpdateGraphRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Layers, Map, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react"; 
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Toolbelt, ToolMode } from "./Toolbelt";
import { VectorComposer } from "./VectorComposer";
import { EdgeData, NodeData } from "./vector/types";

import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";

export function GraphEditorComposer({ floorPlanId }: { floorPlanId: string }) {
  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<ToolMode>("SELECT");
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(true);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  
  const [alertMessage, setAlertMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const idNum = Number(floorPlanId);
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: graphData, isLoading: isDbLoading } = useGetSpatialGraph(idNum);
  const updateSpatialGraphMutation = useUpdateSpatialGraph();
  const updateFloorPlanMutation = useUpdateFloorPlan();
  const calculateRouteMutation = useCalculateRoute();

  const imageUrl = graphData?.floorPlan?.floorPlanUrl;
  const floorName = graphData?.floorPlan?.name || "Loading Floor Plan...";
  const initialScale = graphData?.floorPlan?.scale ? String(graphData.floorPlan.scale) : "";

  // Progress Bar Simulation
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setProgress((oldProgress) => {
        if (oldProgress >= 100) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          setTimeout(() => setIsProcessing(false), 500); 
          return 100;
        }
        return Math.min(oldProgress + Math.random() * 20, 100);
      });
    }, 300);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  // HYDRATION: Check for imported .echonav transient data
  const importedDataStr = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? sessionStorage.getItem(`echonav_import_${idNum}`) : null;
  const importedData = useMemo(() => {
    if (importedDataStr) {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(importedDataStr);
      } catch (e) {
        return null;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }, [importedDataStr]);

  const handleSaveToDb = async (nodes: NodeData[], edges: EdgeData[]) => {
    try {
      const payload: UpdateGraphRequest = {
        nodes: nodes.map((n) => ({ 
          tempId: String(n.id), 
          x: n.x, 
          y: n.y, 
          label: n.label, 
          isHazard: n.isHazard || false,
          hazardType: n.hazardType,
          ttsVerbalGuidance: n.ttsVerbalGuidance, 
          ttsAudioUrl: n.ttsAudioUrl 
        })),
        edges: edges.map((e) => ({ 
          tempId: String(e.id), 
          source: String(e.source), 
          target: String(e.target), 
          isHazard: e.isHazard || false 
        }))
      };
      
      const result = await updateSpatialGraphMutation.mutateAsync({ floorPlanId: idNum, data: payload });
      
      // Cleanup hydration buffer explicitly after the transient maps hit the database tables
      sessionStorage.removeItem(`echonav_import_${idNum}`);
      setAlertMessage("Vector graph securely saved to database."); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
      
      return result; 
      
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      setAlertMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "An unknown error occurred."); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
      return null;
    }
  };

  const initialNodes: NodeData[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (importedData && importedData.nodes) {
      return importedData.nodes.map((n: any) => ({
        id: String(n.tempId || ""),
        x: n.x || 0,
        y: n.y || 0,
        label: n.label || "",
        isHazard: n.isHazard || false,
        hazardType: n.hazardType,
        ttsVerbalGuidance: n.ttsVerbalGuidance,
        ttsAudioUrl: n.ttsAudioUrl
      }));
    }
    const nodes = graphData?.nodes || [];
    return nodes.map((n) => ({
      id: String(n.id || ""),
      x: n.x || 0,
      y: n.y || 0,
      label: n.label || "",
      isHazard: n.isHazard || false, 
      hazardType: n.hazardType,
      ttsVerbalGuidance: n.ttsVerbalGuidance,
      ttsAudioUrl: n.ttsAudioUrl
    }));
  }, [graphData, importedData]);

  const initialEdges: EdgeData[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (importedData && importedData.edges) {
      return importedData.edges.map((e: any) => ({
        id: String(e.tempId || ""),
        source: String(e.source || ""),
        target: String(e.target || ""),
        isHazard: e.isHazard || false,
        weightM: e.weightM,
        distance: e.distance
      }));
    }
    const edges = graphData?.edges || [];
    return edges.map((e) => ({
      id: String(e.id || ""),
      source: String(e.fromNodeId || ""),
      target: String(e.toNodeId || ""),
      isHazard: e.isHazard || false,
      weightM: e.weightM ?? undefined, 
      distance: e.distance ?? undefined
    }));
  }, [graphData, importedData]);

  const handleUpdateScale = async (newScale: number) => {
    try {
      await updateFloorPlanMutation.mutateAsync({ floorPlanId: idNum, data: { scale: newScale } });
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries(); 
      setAlertMessage("Floor plan scale accurately calibrated."); 
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      setAlertMessage(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "An unknown error occurred."); 
    }
  };

  const handleCalculateRoute = async (startId: string, endId: string) => {
    if (startId.startsWith('n') || endId.startsWith('n')) {
      setAlertMessage("⚠️ Unsaved Nodes Detected!\n\nYou must click 'Save Properties' to save your new nodes before the routing engine can calculate a path."); 
      return null;
    }

    try {
      return await calculateRouteMutation.mutateAsync({
        floorPlanId: idNum,
        startNodeId: Number(startId),
        endNodeId: Number(endId),
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Routing failed:", error);
      setAlertMessage("Failed to calculate route. Please try again."); 
      return null;
    }
  };

  const isFullyLoading = isDbLoading || isProcessing;

  const isSuccess = alertMessage?.includes("securely") || alertMessage?.includes("accurately");
  const isWarning = alertMessage?.includes("⚠️");

  const alertConfig = isSuccess 
    ? { icon: <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />, bg: "bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/50", border: "border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-900", title: "Success" }
    : isWarning
    ? { icon: <AlertTriangle className="h-6 w-6 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />, bg: "bg-amber-100 dark:bg-amber-950/50", border: "border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900", title: "Action Required" }
    : { icon: <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />, bg: "bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950/50", border: "border-red-200 dark:border-red-900", title: "System Alert" };

  return (
    <div className="h-full flex flex-col relative rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden  dark:bg-zinc-950">
      <div className="h-14 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 flex items-center justify-between px-4 z-10 shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Map className="h-5 w-5 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400" />
          <span className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100">
            {floorName}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isFullyLoading && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-50 bg-zinc-950 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 flex items-center justify-center mb-6 border-2 border-zinc-700 ">
            <Layers className="h-8 w-8 text-white animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-white mb-2 uppercase">Fetching Vector Map</h2>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-400 mb-8 max-w-sm text-center">
            Pulling architectural data and preparing the spatial grid structure. Please wait.
          </p>
          <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-2">
            <Progress value={progress} className="h-3 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 border-2 border-zinc-700" />
            <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-black text-zinc-500 tracking-widest">
              <span>INITIALIZING</span>
              <span>{Math.round(progress)}%</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex-1 flex overflow-hidden">
        <Toolbelt activeTool={activeTool} setActiveTool={setActiveTool} />
        {!isFullyLoading && (
          <VectorComposer 
            activeTool={activeTool} 
            imageUrl={imageUrl} 
            onSaveDb={handleSaveToDb}
            onUpdateScale={handleUpdateScale}
            onCalculateRoute={handleCalculateRoute}
            initialNodes={initialNodes}
            initialEdges={initialEdges}
            initialScale={initialScale} 
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <AlertDialog open={!!alertMessage} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setAlertMessage(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-6 sm:max-w-[425px]">
          
          <AlertDialogHeader className="flex flex-row items-center gap-4 space-y-0">
            {/* DYNAMIC ICON & BACKGROUND */}
            <div className={`${alertConfig.bg} p-2.5 rounded-lg border-2 ${alertConfig.border} shrink-0`}>
              {alertConfig.icon}
            </div>
            {/* DYNAMIC TITLE */}
            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 m-0">
              {alertConfig.title}
            </AlertDialogTitle>
          </AlertDialogHeader>

          <div className="py-2">
            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">
              <span className="text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200">{alertMessage}</span>
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </div>

          <AlertDialogFooter className="mt-2">
            <AlertDialogAction 
              onClick={() => setAlertMessage(null)}
              className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white font-black uppercase text-sm tracking-wider h-11 px-8 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              Acknowledge
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
          
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}

====================================================================================================

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { ToolMode } from "../Toolbelt";
import { EdgeData, NodeData } from "../vector/types";

interface UseCanvasKeyboardEventsProps {
  state: any; // The state object returned from useEditorState
  nodes: NodeData[];
  edges: EdgeData[];
  pushHistory: (n: NodeData[], e: EdgeData[]) => void;
  undo: () => void;
  redo: () => void;
}

export function useCanvasKeyboardEvents({ state, nodes, edges, pushHistory, undo, redo }: UseCanvasKeyboardEventsProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleDeleteSelection = () => {
      if (state.selection === "NODE" && state.selectedNodeIds.length > 0) {
        const newNodes = nodes.filter((n) => !state.selectedNodeIds.includes(n.id));
        const newEdges = edges.filter((e) => !state.selectedNodeIds.includes(e.source) && !state.selectedNodeIds.includes(e.target));
        pushHistory(newNodes, newEdges);
        state.clearSelection();
      } else if (state.selection === "EDGE" && state.selectedEdgeIds.length > 0) {
        const newEdges = edges.filter((e) => !state.selectedEdgeIds.includes(e.id));
        pushHistory(nodes, newEdges);
        state.clearSelection();
      }
    };

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      const isTypingInInput = target.tagName === "INPUT" || target.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || target.isContentEditable;

      if (e.code === "Space" && target === document.body) {
        e.preventDefault();
        state.setIsSpacePressed(true);
      }
      
      if (!isTypingInInput) {
        if (e.code === "Delete" || e.code === "Backspace") {
          handleDeleteSelection();
        }
        
        if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === "z") {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (e.shiftKey) redo();
          else undo();
        }
      }
    };

    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.code === "Space") state.setIsSpacePressed(false);
    };
    
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    window.addEventListener("keyup", handleKeyUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      window.removeEventListener("keyup", handleKeyUp);
    };
  }, [state, nodes, edges, pushHistory, undo, redo]);
}

import { RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { EdgeData, NodeData } from "../vector/types";

interface PointerEventsConfig {
  state: any;
  nodes: NodeData[];
  edges: EdgeData[];
  pan: { x: number; y: number };
  setPan: (pan: { x: number; y: number }) => void;
  scale: number;
  getLogicalCoords: (x: number, y: number) => { x: number; y: number };
  pushHistory: (n: NodeData[], e: EdgeData[]) => void;
  isUncalibrated: boolean;
  onCalculateRoute?: (startId: string, endId: string) => Promise<RouteResponse | null>;
}

export function useCanvasPointerEvents({
  state, nodes, edges, pan, setPan, scale, getLogicalCoords, pushHistory, isUncalibrated, onCalculateRoute
}: PointerEventsConfig) {

  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    const logical = getLogicalCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);

    // Right Click Box Selection
    if (e.button === 2) {
      state.setSelectionBox({ startX: logical.x, startY: logical.y, currentX: logical.x, currentY: logical.y });
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      return;
    }

    // Panning
    if (e.button === 1 || state.isSpacePressed || (state.activeTool === "SELECT" && e.shiftKey) || state.activeTool === "PAN") {
      state.setIsPanning(true);
      state.setPanStart({ x: e.clientX - pan.x, y: e.clientY - pan.y });
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      return;
    }

    // Calibration
    if (state.activeTool === "SCALE") {
      if (!state.calibrationStart || (state.calibrationStart && state.calibrationEnd)) {
        state.setCalibrationStart({ x: logical.x, y: logical.y });
        state.setCalibrationEnd(null);
        state.setCalibrationCurrent({ x: logical.x, y: logical.y });
      } else if (state.calibrationStart && !state.calibrationEnd) {
        state.setCalibrationEnd({ x: logical.x, y: logical.y });
        state.setCalibrationCurrent(null);
      }
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      return;
    }

    if (isUncalibrated) return;

    // Node Interaction
    const clickedNode = nodes.find((n) => {
      const dx = n.x - logical.x;
      const dy = n.y - (16 / scale) - logical.y;
      return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < 24 / scale;
    });

    if (state.activeTool === "ROUTE") {
      if (clickedNode) {
        if (!state.routeStartNode || (state.routeStartNode && state.routeEndNode)) {
          state.setRouteStartNode(clickedNode.id);
          state.setRouteEndNode(null);
          state.setCalculatedRoute(null);
        } else if (state.routeStartNode && !state.routeEndNode && state.routeStartNode !== clickedNode.id) {
          state.setRouteEndNode(clickedNode.id);
          if (onCalculateRoute) {
            state.setIsCalculatingRoute(true);
            onCalculateRoute(state.routeStartNode, clickedNode.id)
              .then(res => state.setCalculatedRoute(res))
              .catch(err => console.error("Routing error:", err))
              .finally(() => state.setIsCalculatingRoute(false));
          }
        }
      }
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      return;
    }

    if (clickedNode) {
      if (state.activeTool === "SELECT") {
        const newSelection = [clickedNode.id];
        state.setSelectedNodeIds(newSelection);
        state.setSelectedEdgeIds([]);
        state.setSelection("NODE");
        state.setDragStartNodes(nodes.filter((n) => newSelection.includes(n.id)).map((n) => ({ id: n.id, startX: n.x, startY: n.y })));
        state.setActiveDragNodes(nodes); 
        state.setDragStartPos({ x: logical.x, y: logical.y });
      } else if (state.activeTool === "EDGE") {
        state.setDraftEdge({ source: clickedNode.id, currentX: clickedNode.x, currentY: clickedNode.y });
      } else if (state.activeTool === "HAZARD") {
        pushHistory(nodes.map((n) => (n.id === clickedNode.id ? { ...n, isHazard: !n.isHazard } : n)), edges);
      }
      e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      return;
    }

    if (state.activeTool === "NODE") {
      const newNode = { id: `n${Date.now()}`, x: logical.x, y: logical.y, label: `Node ${nodes.length + 1}` };
      pushHistory([...nodes, newNode], edges);
      state.setSelectedNodeIds([newNode.id]);
      state.setSelectedEdgeIds([]);
      state.setSelection("NODE");
    } else if (state.activeTool === "SELECT") {
      state.clearSelection();
    }
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    const logical = getLogicalCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    if (state.isPanning) return setPan({ x: e.clientX - state.panStart.x, y: e.clientY - state.panStart.y });
    if (state.selectionBox) return state.setSelectionBox({ ...state.selectionBox, currentX: logical.x, currentY: logical.y });
    
    if (state.activeTool === "SCALE" && state.calibrationStart && !state.calibrationEnd) {
      state.setCalibrationCurrent({ x: logical.x, y: logical.y });
    }

    if (state.dragStartNodes && state.dragStartPos) {
      const deltaX = logical.x - state.dragStartPos.x;
      const deltaY = logical.y - state.dragStartPos.y;
      state.setActiveDragNodes(
        nodes.map((n) => {
          const startNode = state.dragStartNodes?.find((d: { id: string; }) => d.id === n.id);
          return startNode ? { ...n, x: startNode.startX + deltaX, y: startNode.startY + deltaY } : n;
        })
      );
    }
    if (state.draftEdge) state.setDraftEdge({ ...state.draftEdge, currentX: logical.x, currentY: logical.y });
  };

  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    state.setIsPanning(false);
    if (state.selectionBox) {
      const minX = Math.min(state.selectionBox.startX, state.selectionBox.currentX);
      const maxX = Math.max(state.selectionBox.startX, state.selectionBox.currentX);
      const minY = Math.min(state.selectionBox.startY, state.selectionBox.currentY);
      const maxY = Math.max(state.selectionBox.startY, state.selectionBox.currentY);
      const insideNodes = nodes.filter((n) => n.x >= minX && n.x <= maxX && n.y >= minY && n.y <= maxY).map((n) => n.id);
      
      state.setSelectedNodeIds(insideNodes);
      state.setSelectedEdgeIds([]);
      state.setSelection(insideNodes.length > 0 ? "NODE" : null);
      state.setSelectionBox(null);
      return;
    }

    if (state.dragStartNodes && state.activeDragNodes) {
      pushHistory(state.activeDragNodes, edges);
      state.setDragStartNodes(null);
      state.setDragStartPos(null);
      state.setActiveDragNodes(null);
    }

    if (state.draftEdge) {
      const logical = getLogicalCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
      const dropTarget = nodes.find((n) => {
        const dx = n.x - logical.x;
        const dy = n.y - (16 / scale) - logical.y;
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < 30 / scale;
      });

      if (dropTarget && dropTarget.id !== state.draftEdge.source) {
        const exists = edges.some((edge) => 
          (edge.source === state.draftEdge?.source && edge.target === dropTarget.id) || 
          (edge.target === state.draftEdge?.source && edge.source === dropTarget.id)
        );
        if (!exists) {
          const newEdges = [...edges, { id: `e${Date.now()}`, source: state.draftEdge.source, target: dropTarget.id }];
          pushHistory(nodes, newEdges);
          state.setSelectedEdgeIds([newEdges[newEdges.length - 1].id]);
          state.setSelectedNodeIds([]);
          state.setSelection("EDGE");
        }
      }
      state.setDraftEdge(null);
    }
  };

  const handleEdgeClick = (e: React.PointerEvent, edgeId: string) => {
    const logical = getLogicalCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);

    // THE FIX: Check if the edge click physically overlaps a node's hit-box.
    // If it does, we DO NOT stop propagation, allowing the event to bubble to handlePointerDown for node selection.
    const clickedNode = nodes.find((n) => {
      const dx = n.x - logical.x;
      const dy = n.y - (16 / scale) - logical.y;
      return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < 24 / scale;
    });

    if (clickedNode) {
      return; 
    }

    e.stopPropagation();

    if (state.activeTool === "HAZARD") {
      pushHistory(nodes, edges.map((ed) => (ed.id === edgeId ? { ...ed, isHazard: !ed.isHazard } : ed)));
    } else {
      state.setSelectedEdgeIds([edgeId]);
      state.setSelectedNodeIds([]);
      state.setSelection("EDGE");
    }
  };

  return { handlePointerDown, handlePointerMove, handlePointerUp, handleEdgeClick };
}
