import { RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { SelectionType } from "../InspectorPanel";
import { ToolMode } from "../Toolbelt";
import { NodeData } from "../vector/types";

export function useEditorState(initialTool: ToolMode = "SELECT") {
  // Tool & Selection State
  const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<ToolMode>(initialTool);
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState<SelectionType>(null);
  const [selectedNodeIds, setSelectedNodeIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedEdgeIds, setSelectedEdgeIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  // Keyboard & Panning State
  const [isSpacePressed, setIsSpacePressed] = useState(false);
  const [isPanning, setIsPanning] = useState(false);
  const [panStart, setPanStart] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  // Dragging State
  const [dragStartPos, setDragStartPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const [dragStartNodes, setDragStartNodes] = useState<{ id: string; startX: number; startY: number }[] | null>(null);
  const [activeDragNodes, setActiveDragNodes] = useState<NodeData[] | null>(null);
  
  // Drawing State
  const [draftEdge, setDraftEdge] = useState<{ source: string; currentX: number; currentY: number } | null>(null);
  const [selectionBox, setSelectionBox] = useState<{ startX: number; startY: number; currentX: number; currentY: number } | null>(null);

  // Calibration State
  const [calibrationStart, setCalibrationStart] = useState<{ x: number, y: number } | null>(null);
  const [calibrationEnd, setCalibrationEnd] = useState<{ x: number, y: number } | null>(null);
  const [calibrationCurrent, setCalibrationCurrent] = useState<{ x: number, y: number } | null>(null);

  // Routing State
  const [routeStartNode, setRouteStartNode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [routeEndNode, setRouteEndNode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [calculatedRoute, setCalculatedRoute] = useState<RouteResponse | null>(null);
  const [isCalculatingRoute, setIsCalculatingRoute] = useState(false);

  // Helper clear functions
  const clearSelection = useCallback(() => {
    setSelectedNodeIds([]);
    setSelectedEdgeIds([]);
    setSelection(null);
  }, []);

  const clearCalibration = useCallback(() => {
    setCalibrationStart(null);
    setCalibrationEnd(null);
    setCalibrationCurrent(null);
  }, []);

  const clearRouting = useCallback(() => {
    setRouteStartNode(null);
    setRouteEndNode(null);
    setCalculatedRoute(null);
  }, []);

  return {
    activeTool, setActiveTool,
    selection, setSelection, clearSelection,
    selectedNodeIds, setSelectedNodeIds,
    selectedEdgeIds, setSelectedEdgeIds,
    isSpacePressed, setIsSpacePressed,
    isPanning, setIsPanning,
    panStart, setPanStart,
    dragStartPos, setDragStartPos,
    dragStartNodes, setDragStartNodes,
    activeDragNodes, setActiveDragNodes,
    draftEdge, setDraftEdge,
    selectionBox, setSelectionBox,
    calibrationStart, setCalibrationStart,
    calibrationEnd, setCalibrationEnd,
    calibrationCurrent, setCalibrationCurrent, clearCalibration,
    routeStartNode, setRouteStartNode,
    routeEndNode, setRouteEndNode,
    calculatedRoute, setCalculatedRoute,
    isCalculatingRoute, setIsCalculatingRoute, clearRouting
  };
}

====================================================================================================

import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { EdgeData, NodeData } from "../vector/types";

export function useHistory(initialNodes: NodeData[], initialEdges: EdgeData[]) {
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<{ nodes: NodeData[]; edges: EdgeData[] }[]>([
    { nodes: initialNodes, edges: initialEdges }
  ]);
  const [historyIndex, setHistoryIndex] = useState(0);

  const pushHistory = useCallback((newNodes: NodeData[], newEdges: EdgeData[]) => {
    setHistory((prev) => {
      const newHistory = prev.slice(0, historyIndex + 1);
      newHistory.push({ nodes: newNodes, edges: newEdges });
      return newHistory;
    });
    setHistoryIndex((prev) => prev + 1);
  }, [historyIndex]);

  const undo = useCallback(() => { 
    if (historyIndex > 0) setHistoryIndex((prev) => prev - 1); 
  }, [historyIndex]);

  const redo = useCallback(() => { 
    if (historyIndex < history.length - 1) setHistoryIndex((prev) => prev + 1); 
  }, [historyIndex, history.length]);

  const resetHistory = useCallback((nodes: NodeData[], edges: EdgeData[]) => {
    setHistory([{ nodes, edges }]);
    setHistoryIndex(0);
  }, []);

  const currentHistory = history[historyIndex] || { nodes: [], edges: [] };

  return {
    nodes: currentHistory.nodes,
    edges: currentHistory.edges,
    pushHistory,
    undo,
    redo,
    resetHistory,
    canUndo: historyIndex > 0,
    canRedo: historyIndex < history.length - 1
  };
}

import { RefObject, useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

export function useViewport(containerRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>) {
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 100, y: 100 });
  const [scale, setScale] = useState(1);
  
  // Refs to bypass stale closures in passive event listeners
  const panRef = useRef(pan);
  const scaleRef = useRef(scale);

  useEffect(() => { panRef.current = pan; }, [pan]);
  useEffect(() => { scaleRef.current = scale; }, [scale]);

  // Native wheel event listener for high-performance trackpad zooming
  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    if (!container) return;

    const handleWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
        const zoomFactor = e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.9 : 1.1;
        const newScale = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(scaleRef.current * zoomFactor, 5));
        const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
        
        setScale(newScale);
        setPan({
          x: x - (x - panRef.current.x) * (newScale / scaleRef.current),
          y: y - (y - panRef.current.y) * (newScale / scaleRef.current),
        });
      } else {
        setPan((prev) => ({ x: prev.x - e.deltaX, y: prev.y - e.deltaY }));
      }
    };
    
    container.addEventListener("wheel", handleWheel, { passive: false });
    return () => container.removeEventListener("wheel", handleWheel);
  }, [containerRef]);

  // Button-driven zoom logic
  const adjustZoomCentered = useCallback((factor: number) => {
    const rect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect) return;
    const newScale = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(scale * factor, 5));
    setPan(prevPan => ({
      x: rect.width / 2 - (rect.width / 2 - prevPan.x) * (newScale / scale),
      y: rect.height / 2 - (rect.height / 2 - prevPan.y) * (newScale / scale),
    }));
    setScale(newScale);
  }, [scale, containerRef]);

  const resetZoom = useCallback(() => {
    setScale(1);
    setPan({ x: 100, y: 100 });
  }, []);

  // Logical coordinate projection for mouse clicks
  const getLogicalCoords = useCallback((clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
    const rect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!rect) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
    return {
      x: (clientX - rect.left - pan.x) / scale,
      y: (clientY - rect.top - pan.y) / scale,
    };
  }, [pan, scale, containerRef]);

  return { 
    pan, 
    setPan, 
    scale, 
    setScale, 
    adjustZoomCentered, 
    resetZoom, 
    getLogicalCoords 
  };
}

"use client";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useDeleteNode } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";
import { RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { MapPin, Navigation, Route, Ruler } from "lucide-react";
import { ToolMode } from "./Toolbelt";
import { NodeData } from "./vector/types";

// Import Modularized Panels
import { EdgeInspector } from "./inspectors/EdgeInspector";
import { EmptyState } from "./inspectors/EmptyState";
import { NodeInspector } from "./inspectors/NodeInspector";
import { RouteInspector } from "./inspectors/RouteInspector";
import { ScaleCalibration } from "./inspectors/ScaleCalibration";

export type SelectionType = "NODE" | "EDGE" | null;

interface InspectorProps {
  selectionType: SelectionType;
  activeTool: ToolMode;
  selectedNode?: NodeData;
  floorPlanScale: string;
  updateNode?: (updates: Partial<NodeData>) => void;
  onSave?: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  calibrationPixelDistance?: number | null;
  onCalibrate?: (scale: number) => void;
  edgeMeters?: number | null;
  routeStartNode?: string | null;
  routeEndNode?: string | null;
  calculatedRoute?: RouteResponse | null;
  isCalculatingRoute?: boolean;
  onResolveHazard?: (nodeId: string) => void;
}

export function InspectorPanel({ 
  selectionType, activeTool, selectedNode, updateNode, onSave, onDelete, calibrationPixelDistance, onCalibrate, edgeMeters, routeStartNode, routeEndNode, calculatedRoute, isCalculatingRoute, onResolveHazard 
}: InspectorProps) {
  const isScaling = activeTool === "SCALE";
  const isRouting = activeTool === "ROUTE";
  const isNode = selectionType === "NODE";
  const isEdge = selectionType === "EDGE";

  const deleteNodeMutation = useDeleteNode();
  const handleNodeDelete = () => {
    if (selectedNode?.id && (typeof selectedNode.id === 'number' || !String(selectedNode.id).startsWith('n'))) {
       deleteNodeMutation.mutate(Number(selectedNode.id));
    }
    onDelete(); 
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-80 shrink-0 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-l-2 border-zinc-950 flex flex-col z-10">
      <div className="p-5 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 flex items-center gap-4 bg-zinc-50/50">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 bg-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          {isScaling ? <Ruler className="h-5 w-5" /> : isRouting ? <Navigation className="h-5 w-5 text-purple-600" /> : isNode ? <MapPin className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Route className="h-5 w-5" />}
        </div>
        <h3 className="font-black text-sm uppercase">{isScaling ? "Scale" : isRouting ? "Path Simulation" : isNode ? "Node" : "Path"}</h3>
      </div>

      {isScaling ? (
        <ScaleCalibration pixelDistance={calibrationPixelDistance} onCalibrate={onCalibrate} />
      ) : isRouting ? (
        <RouteInspector start={routeStartNode} end={routeEndNode} route={calculatedRoute} isCalculating={isCalculatingRoute} />
      ) : isNode ? (
        <NodeInspector node={selectedNode} updateNode={updateNode} onSave={onSave} onResolveHazard={onResolveHazard} />
      ) : isEdge ? (
        <EdgeInspector meters={edgeMeters} />
      ) : (
        <EmptyState />
      )}

      {(isNode || isEdge) && !isScaling && !isRouting && (
        <div className="p-5 border-t-2 border-zinc-950 bg-zinc-50 space-y-3 mt-auto">
          <Button variant="destructive" onClick={isNode ? handleNodeDelete : onDelete} className="w-full border-2 border-zinc-950">Delete</Button>
          <Button onClick={onSave} className="w-full bg-zinc-950 text-white border-2 border-zinc-950">Save Properties</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle, AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { useDeleteNodeTts, useGenerateNodeTts } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";
import { CheckCircle2, Loader2, Mic, Play, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { NodeData } from "../vector/types";

export function NodeInspector({ node, updateNode, onSave, onResolveHazard }: { node?: NodeData | any, updateNode?: (u: Partial<NodeData>) => void, onSave?: () => void, onResolveHazard?: (id: string) => void }) {
  const [ttsInput, setTtsInput] = useState(node?.ttsVerbalGuidance || "");
  const [audioVersion, setAudioVersion] = useState(Date.now());
  const generateMutation = useGenerateNodeTts();
  const deleteTtsMutation = useDeleteNodeTts();

  useEffect(() => {
    setTtsInput(node?.ttsVerbalGuidance || "");
    setAudioVersion(Date.now());
  }, [node?.id, node?.ttsAudioUrl]);

  const handleGenerate = () => {
    if (!node?.id) return;
    generateMutation.mutate({ nodeId: Number(node.id), text: ttsInput }, {
      onSuccess: (updatedNodeFromDb) => {
        if (updateNode) updateNode({ ttsAudioUrl: updatedNodeFromDb.ttsAudioUrl });
      }
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteTts = () => {
    if (!node?.id) return;
    deleteTtsMutation.mutate(Number(node.id), {
      onSuccess: () => {
        setTtsInput("");
        if (updateNode) updateNode({ ttsVerbalGuidance: undefined, ttsAudioUrl: undefined } as any);
      }
    });
  };

  const isGenerating = generateMutation.isPending;
  const hasAudio = !!node?.ttsAudioUrl;
  const isInputDirty = ttsInput !== node?.ttsVerbalGuidance;
  const isNewNode = !node?.id || String(node.id).startsWith("n");

  const apiUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8081";
  const baseAudioUrl = node?.ttsAudioUrl ? (node.ttsAudioUrl.startsWith('/') ? `${apiUrl}${node.ttsAudioUrl}` : node.ttsAudioUrl) : "";
  const fullAudioUrl = baseAudioUrl ? `${baseAudioUrl}?v=${audioVersion}` : "";

  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="p-5 space-y-6">
        
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <Label className="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-widest">Node Label (Name)</Label>
          <Input 
            value={node?.label || ""} 
            onChange={(e) => updateNode?.({ label: e.target.value })} 
            onBlur={onSave} 
            placeholder="e.g., Lobby Entrance" 
            className="text-sm font-bold bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl h-11 focus-visible:ring-0  dark:shadow-none" 
          />
        </div>
        
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <Label className="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-widest">Verbal Guidance (TTS)</Label>
          <Textarea 
            value={ttsInput}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setTtsInput(e.target.value);
              updateNode?.({ ttsVerbalGuidance: e.target.value } as any);
            }}
            placeholder="e.g., You are passing the Science Lab on your right." 
            className="resize-none h-24 text-sm font-medium bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl focus-visible:ring-0 dark:shadow-none p-3" 
          />
          
          {isNewNode ? (
            <div className="p-3 mt-2 border-2 border-amber-500 bg-amber-50 rounded-xl text-center">
               <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-700 mb-2">Save properties to register this node before generating audio.</p>
               <Button onClick={onSave} className="w-full bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-700 text-white font-bold">Save Properties Now</Button>
            </div>
          ) : !hasAudio || isInputDirty ? (
            <Button onClick={handleGenerate} disabled={isGenerating || !ttsInput.trim()} className="w-full mt-2 bg-zinc-950 text-white hover:bg-zinc-800">
              {isGenerating ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin mr-2" /> : <Mic className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />}
              {isGenerating ? "Generating..." : "Generate Audio"}
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
              <audio src={fullAudioUrl} className="hidden" id={`audio-${node.id}`} />
              <Button 
                variant="secondary" 
                className="flex-1 border-2 border-zinc-950" 
                onClick={() => {
                  const audioEl = document.getElementById(`audio-${node.id}`) as HTMLAudioElement;
                  if (audioEl) {
                    audioEl.load();
                    audioEl.play();
                  }
                }}
              >
                <Play className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Play Audio
              </Button>
              <AlertDialog>
                <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                  <Button variant="destructive" size="icon" className="border-2 border-zinc-950"><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button>
                </AlertDialogTrigger>
                <AlertDialogContent>
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Are you absolutely sure?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>Are you sure you want to delete this verbal guidance audio? This cannot be undone.</AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel>Cancel</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction onClick={handleDeleteTts} className="bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white">Delete</AlertDialogAction>
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-4 border-t-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <Label className="text-[10px] font-black text-rose-600 uppercase tracking-widest">Mark as Hazard</Label>
            <Switch 
              checked={node?.isHazard || false} 
              onCheckedChange={(c) => updateNode?.({ isHazard: c, hazardType: c ? node?.hazardType || "Fire" : undefined } as any)} 
              className="data-[state=checked]:bg-rose-500 border-2 border-zinc-950 m-0"
            />
          </div>

          {node?.isHazard && (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2">
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <Label className="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-widest">Hazard Type</Label>
                <Select value={node?.hazardType || "Fire"} onValueChange={(val) => updateNode?.({ hazardType: val } as any)}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="h-11 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 font-bold  dark:shadow-none">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Select type..." />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 rounded-xl font-bold">
                    <SelectItem value="Fire">Fire</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="Spill">Spill</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="Obstruction">Obstruction</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="Maintenance">Maintenance</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              <AlertDialog>
                <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                  <Button variant="outline" className="w-full h-11 border-2 border-emerald-500 text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/30 font-bold  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" /> Resolve Hazard
                  </Button>
                </AlertDialogTrigger>
                <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl  dark:shadow-none">
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-tight">Resolve Hazard?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-500">
                      Are you sure this hazard has been resolved? This will clear the hazard from the map and update the system logs.
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 font-bold uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10  dark:shadow-none">Cancel</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction 
                      onClick={() => { if (node?.id) onResolveHazard?.(node.id); }}
                      className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 dark:text-black text-white font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10 border-2 border-zinc-950  dark:shadow-none"
                    >
                      Confirm & Resolve
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { NodeDto, RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { Clock, Zap } from "lucide-react";

export function RouteInspector({ start, end, route, isCalculating }: { start?: string | null, end?: string | null, route?: RouteResponse | null, isCalculating?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
      <div className="p-5 space-y-6">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <Label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-zinc-500">Route Waypoints</Label>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className={`p-3 border-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold truncate ${start ? 'border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 dark:bg-purple-900/20 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300' : 'border-zinc-950 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900'}`}>
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase opacity-70 block mb-0.5">Start</span>
              {start || "Click a starting node..."}
            </div>
            <div className={`p-3 border-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold truncate ${end ? 'border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 dark:bg-purple-900/20 text-purple-700 dark:text-purple-300' : 'border-zinc-950 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900'}`}>
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase opacity-70 block mb-0.5">Destination</span>
              {end || "Click an ending node..."}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {isCalculating && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center p-4 border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 rounded-xl bg-zinc-50">
            <Zap className="h-5 w-5 text-purple-500 animate-bounce mr-2" />
            <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-purple-700">Running Dijkstra...</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {route && !isCalculating && route.status !== "NO_PATH_FOUND" && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-2">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
               <div className="bg-purple-600 text-white p-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 ">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest opacity-80 mb-1">Distance</div>
                  <div className="text-lg font-black">{route.totalDistanceMeters.toFixed(1)}m</div>
               </div>
               <div className="bg-zinc-950 text-white p-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 ">
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest opacity-80 mb-1 flex items-center"><Clock className="w-3 h-3 mr-1"/> ETA</div>
                  <div className="text-lg font-black">{Math.ceil(route.estimatedTimeSeconds)}s</div>
               </div>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2 mt-4">
              <Label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase text-zinc-500">Turn-by-Turn</Label>
              <div className="max-h-48 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 pr-1 custom-scrollbar">
                {route.path.map((step: NodeDto, idx: number) => (
                  <div key={idx} className="bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 p-2.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex gap-3">
                    <span className="bg-zinc-950 text-white w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">{idx + 1}</span>
                    <span className="mt-0.5">{step.ttsVerbalGuidance || `Proceed to ${step.label}`}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {route && route.status === "NO_PATH_FOUND" && (
            <div className="p-4 border-2 border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-center">
              No continuous path exists between these points.
            </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\components\map-studio\editor\inspectors\ScaleCalibration.tsx
====================================================================================================

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { useState } from "react";

export function ScaleCalibration({ pixelDistance, onCalibrate }: { pixelDistance?: number | null; onCalibrate?: (s: number) => void; }) {
  const [meters, setMeters] = useState("");
  
  const handleCalibrate = () => {
    if (!pixelDistance || !meters) return;
    const parsedMeters = parseFloat(meters);
    if (isNaN(parsedMeters) || parsedMeters <= 0) return;
    onCalibrate?.(parsedMeters / pixelDistance);
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-5 space-y-6">
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase">Raw Pixel Distance</Label>
        <div className="text-xl font-black bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-2 border-zinc-950 p-3 text-center rounded-xl">
          {pixelDistance ? `${pixelDistance.toFixed(2)} px` : "Draw a line..."}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <Label className="text-[10px] font-black uppercase">Real-World Distance (Meters)</Label>
        <Input type="number" value={meters} onChange={(e) => setMeters(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g., 1.5" className="text-center font-bold border-2 border-zinc-950 rounded-xl h-12" />
      </div>
      <Button onClick={handleCalibrate} disabled={!pixelDistance || !meters} className="w-full bg-indigo-600 text-white font-black uppercase h-12 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950">Calibrate</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { AlertTriangle, ArrowLeft, Hand, MapPin, MousePointer2, Navigation, Route, Ruler } from "lucide-react";

export type ToolMode = "SELECT" | "PAN" | "NODE" | "EDGE" | "HAZARD" | "SCALE" | "ROUTE";

export function Toolbelt({ activeTool, setActiveTool }: { activeTool: ToolMode, setActiveTool: (t: ToolMode) => void }) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const tools = [
    { id: "SELECT", icon: MousePointer2, label: "Select (V)" },
    { id: "PAN", icon: Hand, label: "Pan (Space)" },
    { id: "NODE", icon: MapPin, label: "Add Node (N)" },
    { id: "EDGE", icon: Route, label: "Connect (E)" },
    { id: "HAZARD", icon: AlertTriangle, label: "Toggle Hazard (H)" },
    { id: "SCALE", icon: Ruler, label: "Calibrate Scale (C)" },
    { id: "ROUTE", icon: Navigation, label: "Simulate Route (R)" },
  ] as const;

  return (
    <div className="w-16 bg-zinc-950 flex flex-col items-center py-4 gap-2 border-r-2 border-zinc-800 z-20 shrink-0">
      
      {/* Navigation Action */}
      <div className="w-full flex justify-center border-b-2 border-zinc-800 pb-4 mb-2">
        <button
          onClick={() => router.back()}
          title="Go Back"
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all bg-white text-zinc-950  hover:bg-zinc-200"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Existing Canvas Tools */}
      {tools.map((t) => {
        const Icon = t.icon;
        const isActive = activeTool === t.id;
        return (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            onClick={() => setActiveTool(t.id)}
            title={t.label}
            className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all ${
              isActive ? "bg-white text-zinc-950 " : "text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

interface BlueprintLayerProps {
  imageUrl?: string;
}

export function BlueprintLayer({ imageUrl }: BlueprintLayerProps) {
  if (!imageUrl) return null;

  // Use environment variable, fallback to localhost for local dev if missing
  const apiUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "http://localhost:8081";
  const fullUrl = imageUrl.startsWith('/') ? `${apiUrl}${imageUrl}` : imageUrl;

  return (
    <svg className="absolute inset-0 overflow-visible pointer-events-none opacity-40 dark:opacity-60">
        <image 
          href={fullUrl} 
          x="0" 
          y="0" 
          width="3000" 
          height="3000" 
          preserveAspectRatio="xMinYMin meet" 
        />
    </svg>
  );
}

import { NodeDto, RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { SelectionType } from "../InspectorPanel";
import { EdgeData, NodeData } from "./types";

interface EdgeLayerProps {
  nodes: NodeData[];
  edges: EdgeData[];
  selectedEdgeIds: string[];
  selection: SelectionType;
  draftEdge: { source: string; currentX: number; currentY: number } | null;
  selectionBox: { startX: number; startY: number; currentX: number; currentY: number } | null;
  scale: number;
  floorPlanScale: number; 
  onEdgeClick: (e: React.PointerEvent, edgeId: string) => void;
  calibrationStart?: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  calibrationEnd?: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  calibrationCurrent?: { x: number; y: number } | null;
  calculatedRoute?: RouteResponse | null;
}

export function EdgeLayer({ 
  nodes, edges, selectedEdgeIds, selection, draftEdge, selectionBox, scale, floorPlanScale, onEdgeClick,
  calibrationStart, calibrationEnd, calibrationCurrent, calculatedRoute
}: EdgeLayerProps) {
  return (
    <svg className="absolute inset-0 overflow-visible pointer-events-none">
      {edges.map(edge => {
        const source = nodes.find(n => n.id === edge.source);
        const target = nodes.find(n => n.id === edge.target);
        if (!source || !target) return null;
        
        const isSelected = selectedEdgeIds.includes(edge.id) && selection === "EDGE";
        const isHazard = edge.isHazard;
        
        const pixelDistance = Math.hypot(target.x - source.x, target.y - source.y);
        const realtimeWeightM = !isNaN(floorPlanScale) ? (pixelDistance * floorPlanScale) : null;
        const displayWeight = realtimeWeightM ? `${realtimeWeightM.toFixed(1)}m` : 'Uncalibrated';
        
        return (
          <g key={edge.id} className="pointer-events-auto cursor-pointer" onPointerDown={(e) => onEdgeClick(e, edge.id)}>
            <line 
                x1={source.x} y1={source.y} x2={target.x} y2={target.y} 
                stroke="transparent" 
                strokeWidth={20 / scale} 
            />
            <line 
                x1={source.x} y1={source.y} x2={target.x} y2={target.y} 
                stroke={isHazard ? "#ef4444" : isSelected ? "#6366f1" : "#10b981"} 
                strokeWidth={(isSelected ? 6 : 4) / scale} 
                strokeDasharray={isHazard ? `${8 / scale} ${8 / scale}` : "none"}
                className="transition-colors hover:stroke-indigo-400" 
            />
            {/* Realtime Label Rendering */}
            <text 
                x={(source.x + target.x) / 2} 
                y={(source.y + target.y) / 2 - (6 / scale)} 
                fontSize={12 / scale} 
                fontWeight="900" 
                textAnchor="middle" 
                fill="#18181b" 
                stroke="#ffffff" 
                strokeWidth={3 / scale} 
                paintOrder="stroke" 
                className="pointer-events-none select-none transition-all"
            >
                {displayWeight}
            </text>
          </g>
        );
      })}
  
      {calculatedRoute && calculatedRoute.path && calculatedRoute.path.length > 0 && (
        <polyline
            points={calculatedRoute.path.map((step: NodeDto) => `${step.x},${step.y}`).join(" ")}
            fill="none"
            stroke="#a855f7"
            strokeWidth={8 / scale}
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            className="animate-pulse"
            style={{ filter: "drop-shadow(0px 0px 8px rgba(168,85,247,0.8))" }}
        />
      )}

      {draftEdge && (
          <line 
              x1={nodes.find(n => n.id === draftEdge.source)?.x || 0} 
              y1={nodes.find(n => n.id === draftEdge.source)?.y || 0} 
              x2={draftEdge.currentX} y2={draftEdge.currentY} 
              stroke="#10b981" 
              strokeWidth={4 / scale} 
              strokeDasharray={`${6 / scale} ${6 / scale}`} 
          />
      )}

      {selectionBox && (
          <rect 
              x={Math.min(selectionBox.startX, selectionBox.currentX)}
              y={Math.min(selectionBox.startY, selectionBox.currentY)}
              width={Math.abs(selectionBox.currentX - selectionBox.startX)}
              height={Math.abs(selectionBox.currentY - selectionBox.startY)}
              fill="rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.15)"
              stroke="#6366f1"
              strokeWidth={2 / scale}
              strokeDasharray={`${4 / scale} ${4 / scale}`}
          />
      )}

      {calibrationStart && (calibrationCurrent || calibrationEnd) && (
          <g className="pointer-events-none">
            <line x1={calibrationStart.x} y1={calibrationStart.y} x2={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.x} y2={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.y} stroke="#0ea5e9" strokeWidth={4 / scale} strokeDasharray={`${8 / scale} ${8 / scale}`} />
            <circle cx={calibrationStart.x} cy={calibrationStart.y} r={8 / scale} fill="none" stroke="#0ea5e9" strokeWidth={2 / scale} />
            <circle cx={calibrationStart.x} cy={calibrationStart.y} r={2 / scale} fill="#0ea5e9" />
            <circle cx={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.x} cy={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.y} r={8 / scale} fill="none" stroke="#0ea5e9" strokeWidth={2 / scale} />
            <circle cx={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.x} cy={(calibrationEnd || calibrationCurrent)!.y} r={2 / scale} fill="#0ea5e9" />
          </g>
      )}
    </svg>
  );
}

import { NodeData } from "./types";

interface NodeLayerProps {

}

export function NodeLayer({ nodes, selectedNodeIds, scale }: NodeLayerProps) {
  return (
    <>
      {nodes.map(node => {
        const isSelected = selectedNodeIds.includes(node.id);

              style={{ 
                left: `${node.x}px`, 
                top: `${node.y}px`,

                transformOrigin: "bottom center",
                zIndex: isSelected ? 50 : 10
              }}
          >

              {node.label}
            </div>
            
            {/* The Teardrop Pin Icon */}
            <div className={`relative flex items-center justify-center transition-transform origin-bottom ${isSelected ? 'scale-125' : 'hover:scale-110'}`}>
                <svg width="28" height="28" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={`drop-shadow-md ${node.isHazard ? 'text-red-500 fill-red-100 dark:fill-red-950' : isSelected ? 'text-indigo-600 fill-indigo-100 dark:fill-indigo-900' : 'text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 fill-white dark:fill-zinc-900'}`}>
                    <path d="M20 10c0 6-8 12-8 12s-8-6-8-12a8 8 0 0 1 16 0Z"></path>
                    <circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3" className={node.isHazard ? "fill-red-500" : isSelected ? "fill-indigo-600" : "fill-zinc-950 dark:fill-zinc-100"}></circle>
                </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
}

import { RouteResponse } from "@/lib/api/types";

    startId: string,

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { InspectorPanel } from "./InspectorPanel";
import { BlueprintLayer } from "./vector/BlueprintLayer";
import { EdgeLayer } from "./vector/EdgeLayer";
import { HistoryControls } from "./vector/HistoryControls";
import { NodeLayer } from "./vector/NodeLayer";
import { StatusBar } from "./vector/StatusBar";
import { VectorComposerProps } from "./vector/types";
import { ZoomControls } from "./vector/ZoomControls";
import { Ruler } from "lucide-react";

import { useEditorState } from "./hooks/useEditorState";
import { useHistory } from "./hooks/useHistory";
import { useViewport } from "./hooks/useViewport";
import { useCanvasKeyboardEvents } from "./hooks/useCanvasKeyboardEvents";
import { useCanvasPointerEvents } from "./hooks/useCanvasPointerEvents";

export function VectorComposer({ 
  activeTool: externalActiveTool, imageUrl, onSaveDb, onUpdateScale, onCalculateRoute,
  initialNodes = [], initialEdges = [], initialScale = ""
}: VectorComposerProps) {
  
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const hasHydrated = useRef(false);
  
  const { pan, setPan, scale, adjustZoomCentered, resetZoom, getLogicalCoords } = useViewport(containerRef);
  const { nodes, edges, pushHistory, undo, redo, canUndo, canRedo, resetHistory } = useHistory(initialNodes, initialEdges);
  const state = useEditorState(externalActiveTool);

  const isUncalibrated = initialScale === "" || initialScale === "0.05";
  const parsedScale = parseFloat(initialScale);

  // 1. Tool Locks & State Resets
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isUncalibrated && state.activeTool !== "SCALE") {
        state.setActiveTool("SCALE");
    } else if (!isUncalibrated && externalActiveTool !== state.activeTool) {
        state.setActiveTool(externalActiveTool);
    }
  }, [isUncalibrated, externalActiveTool, state.activeTool, state]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.activeTool !== "SCALE") state.clearCalibration();
    if (state.activeTool !== "ROUTE") state.clearRouting();
  }, [state.activeTool, state]); 

  // 2. Initial Hydration
  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialNodes.length > 0 && !hasHydrated.current) {
      resetHistory(initialNodes, initialEdges);
      state.clearSelection();
      hasHydrated.current = true;
    }
  }, [initialNodes, initialEdges, resetHistory, state]);

  // 3. Delegate Event Handling to Hooks
  useCanvasKeyboardEvents({ state, nodes, edges, pushHistory, undo, redo });
  
  const { handlePointerDown, handlePointerMove, handlePointerUp, handleEdgeClick } = useCanvasPointerEvents({
    state, nodes, edges, pan, setPan, scale, getLogicalCoords, pushHistory, isUncalibrated, onCalculateRoute
  });

  // 4. Derived Math for Inspector
  const calibrationPixelDistance = useMemo(() => {
    if (state.calibrationStart && state.calibrationEnd) {
      return Math.hypot(state.calibrationEnd.x - state.calibrationStart.x, state.calibrationEnd.y - state.calibrationStart.y);
    }
    return null;
  }, [state.calibrationStart, state.calibrationEnd]);

  const selectedEdgeDistance = useMemo(() => {
    if (state.selection === "EDGE" && state.selectedEdgeIds.length === 1) {
      const edge = edges.find(e => e.id === state.selectedEdgeIds[0]);
      if (edge) {
        const s = nodes.find(n => n.id === edge.source);
        const t = nodes.find(n => n.id === edge.target);
        if (s && t) {
          const pixels = Math.hypot(t.x - s.x, t.y - s.y);
          return isNaN(parsedScale) ? null : pixels * parsedScale;
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }, [state.selection, state.selectedEdgeIds, edges, nodes, parsedScale]);

  // 5. Database Save Handlers
  const handleSaveWrapper = async () => {
    pushHistory(nodes, edges);
    if (onSaveDb) {
      try {
        const updatedGraph = await onSaveDb(nodes, edges);
        if (updatedGraph && updatedGraph.nodes) {
           const freshNodes = updatedGraph.nodes.map((n: any) => ({
             id: String(n.id), x: n.x, y: n.y, label: n.label || "",
             isHazard: n.isHazard || false, ttsVerbalGuidance: n.ttsVerbalGuidance, ttsAudioUrl: n.ttsAudioUrl
           }));
           
           const freshEdges = updatedGraph.edges.map((e: any) => ({
             id: String(e.id), source: String(e.fromNodeId), target: String(e.toNodeId),
             isHazard: e.isHazard || false, weightM: e.weightM, distance: e.distance
           }));
           
           resetHistory(freshNodes, freshEdges);
        }
        state.clearSelection();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Save failed", err);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleResolveHazard = async (nodeId: string) => {
    const newNodes = nodes.map(n => n.id === nodeId ? { ...n, isHazard: false, hazardType: undefined } : n);
    pushHistory(newNodes, edges);
    if (onSaveDb) {
      try { await onSaveDb(newNodes, edges); } 
      catch (err) { console.error("Resolve save failed", err); }
    }
  };

  const handleCalibrateWrapper = async (newScaleRatio: number) => {
    if (onUpdateScale) {
      try { await onUpdateScale(newScaleRatio); } 
      catch (err) { console.error("Scale update failed", err); }
    }
    state.clearCalibration();
  };

  const displayNodes = state.activeDragNodes || nodes;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden relative">
      {isUncalibrated && (
        <div className="absolute top-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 bg-amber-400 text-amber-950 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950  flex items-center gap-3 font-black tracking-wide uppercase text-xs animate-bounce pointer-events-none">
           <Ruler className="h-5 w-5" />
           Calibration Required: Draw a line between two known points to establish real-world scale.
        </div>
      )}

      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        className="flex-1 relative overflow-hidden bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 select-none outline-none"
        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
        onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
        onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
        onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        tabIndex={0}
        style={{
          backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(circle, rgba(161, 161, 170, 0.4) 1.5px, transparent 1.5px)`,
          backgroundSize: `${24 * scale}px ${24 * scale}px`,
          backgroundPosition: `${pan.x}px ${pan.y}px`,
        }}
      >
        <HistoryControls undo={undo} redo={redo} canUndo={canUndo} canRedo={canRedo} />
        <ZoomControls scale={scale} adjustZoomCentered={adjustZoomCentered} resetZoom={resetZoom} />
        <StatusBar activeTool={state.activeTool} nodeCount={nodes.length} />

        <div
          className="absolute inset-0 origin-top-left pointer-events-none"
          style={{ transform: `translate(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px) scale(${scale})` }}
        >
          <BlueprintLayer imageUrl={imageUrl} />
          <EdgeLayer 
            nodes={displayNodes} edges={edges} selectedEdgeIds={state.selectedEdgeIds} 
            selection={state.selection} draftEdge={state.draftEdge} selectionBox={state.selectionBox} 
            scale={scale} floorPlanScale={parsedScale} onEdgeClick={handleEdgeClick} 
            calibrationStart={state.calibrationStart} calibrationEnd={state.calibrationEnd}
            calibrationCurrent={state.calibrationCurrent} calculatedRoute={state.calculatedRoute}
          />
          <NodeLayer nodes={displayNodes} selectedNodeIds={state.selectedNodeIds} scale={scale} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <InspectorPanel
        selectionType={state.selection} activeTool={state.activeTool} floorPlanScale={initialScale} 
        selectedNode={state.selectedNodeIds.length === 1 ? nodes.find((n) => n.id === state.selectedNodeIds[0]) : undefined}
        updateNode={(updates) => {
          if (state.selectedNodeIds.length === 1) {
             pushHistory(nodes.map((n) => n.id === state.selectedNodeIds[0] ? { ...n, ...updates } : n), edges);
          }
        }}
        onSave={handleSaveWrapper}
        onDelete={() => {
            const e = new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { code: "Delete" });
            window.dispatchEvent(e);
        }}
        calibrationPixelDistance={calibrationPixelDistance} onCalibrate={handleCalibrateWrapper} edgeMeters={selectedEdgeDistance} 
        routeStartNode={state.routeStartNode} routeEndNode={state.routeEndNode} calculatedRoute={state.calculatedRoute}
        isCalculatingRoute={state.isCalculatingRoute} onResolveHazard={handleResolveHazard}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { CreateBuilding } from "@/components/map-studio/CreateBuilding";
import { Pagination, PaginationContent, PaginationItem, PaginationNext, PaginationPrevious } from "@/components/ui/pagination";
import { useCreateBuilding, useDeleteBuilding, useGetBuildings } from "@/hooks/api/useMapStudio";
import { Map, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { BuildingCard, BuildingData } from "./BuildingCard";

// SHADCN IMPORTS
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";

const BUILDINGS_PER_PAGE = 6;

export function MapStudioComposer() {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const orgIdNum = activeOrgId ? Number(activeOrgId) : undefined;

  // Pagination State
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  
  // NEW: Alert State
  const [alertMessage, setAlertMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // TanStack Hooks
  const { data: buildingsDto, isLoading, isError } = useGetBuildings(orgIdNum);
  const createBuildingMutation = useCreateBuilding();
  const deleteBuildingMutation = useDeleteBuilding();

  const mappedBuildings: BuildingData[] = (buildingsDto || []).map((b) => ({
    id: String(b.id),
    name: b.name || "Unnamed Building",
    address: b.address || "No Address Provided",
    privacy: (b.privacyLevel === "PRIVATE" ? "PRIVATE" : "PUBLIC"),
    stats: { ActiveFloorPlans: 0 }, 
    health: "ONLINE",
    updatedAt: "Just now",
  }));

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(mappedBuildings.length / BUILDINGS_PER_PAGE));
  const safeCurrentPage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);
  
  const startIndex = (safeCurrentPage - 1) * BUILDINGS_PER_PAGE;
  const paginatedBuildings = mappedBuildings.slice(startIndex, startIndex + BUILDINGS_PER_PAGE);

  const handleAddBuilding = async (newBuilding: Omit<BuildingData, "id" | "stats" | "health" | "updatedAt">) => {
    if (!orgIdNum) return;
    try {
      await createBuildingMutation.mutateAsync({
        orgId: orgIdNum,
        data: {
          name: newBuilding.name,
          address: newBuilding.address,
          privacyLevel: newBuilding.privacy,
        }
      });
      setCurrentPage(1);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) setAlertMessage(error.message); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteBuilding = async (id: string) => {
    if (!orgIdNum) return;
    try {
      await deleteBuildingMutation.mutateAsync({ buildingId: Number(id) });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) setAlertMessage(error.message); // REPLACED NATIVE ALERT
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div className="sticky -top-6 md:-top-14 z-40 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 -mt-6 md:-mt-8 -mx-6 md:-mx-8 px-6 md:px-8 pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-5 mb-8">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase">Map Studio Directory</h1>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">Manage architectural floor plans and spatial routing graphs.</p>
        </div>
        <CreateBuilding onAdd={handleAddBuilding} />
      </div>

      {isLoading && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-6 pb-4">
          {[1, 2, 3].map((skeleton) => (
            <div key={skeleton} className="h-70 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 animate-pulse" />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {isError && (
        <div className="p-6 text-center border-2 border-red-500 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/20 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 rounded-xl font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sm">
          Failed to load directory. Please check your connection.
        </div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && mappedBuildings.length === 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-12 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 rounded-xl bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 mt-8">
          <div className="h-16 w-16 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center mb-4  dark:shadow-none">
            <Map className="h-8 w-8 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-tight">No Buildings Found</h3>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 mt-2 max-w-md">Your organization hasn&apos;t registered any physical infrastructure yet. Click &quot;Add New Building&quot; to start mapping.</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && paginatedBuildings.length > 0 && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-6 pb-4">
          {paginatedBuildings.map((building) => (
            <BuildingCard 
              key={building.id} 
              building={building} 
             orgId={orgIdNum!}
              onDelete={handleDeleteBuilding} 
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {!isLoading && mappedBuildings.length > BUILDINGS_PER_PAGE && (
        <div className="pt-6 pb-8 flex justify-center border-t-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <Pagination>
            <PaginationContent className="gap-2">
              <PaginationItem>
                <PaginationPrevious 
                  href="#" 
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if (safeCurrentPage > 1) setCurrentPage(p => p - 1);
                  }}
                  className={`border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 font-semibold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider h-9 px-3 ${safeCurrentPage === 1 ? "pointer-events-none opacity-50" : "hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 cursor-pointer"}`} 
                />
              </PaginationItem>
              
              <PaginationItem>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-100 bg-zinc-950 text-white dark:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-950 font-bold h-9 px-4 rounded-md text-sm">
                  {safeCurrentPage} / {totalPages}
                </div>
              </PaginationItem>
              
              <PaginationItem>
                <PaginationNext 
                  href="#" 
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    if (safeCurrentPage < totalPages) setCurrentPage(p => p + 1);
                  }}
                  className={`border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 font-semibold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider h-9 px-3 ${safeCurrentPage === totalPages ? "pointer-events-none opacity-50" : "hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 cursor-pointer"}`} 
                />
              </PaginationItem>
            </PaginationContent>
          </Pagination>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SHADCN ALERT DIALOG COMPONENT */}
      <AlertDialog open={!!alertMessage} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setAlertMessage(null)}>
        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl p-6 sm:max-w-[425px]">
          
          <AlertDialogHeader className="flex flex-row items-center gap-4 space-y-0">
            <div className="bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950/50 p-2.5 rounded-lg border-2 border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 shrink-0">
              <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-red-600 dark:text-red-500" strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </div>
            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-xl text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 m-0">
              System Alert
            </AlertDialogTitle>
          </AlertDialogHeader>

          <div className="py-2">
            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed">
              <span className="text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-200">{alertMessage}</span>
              {"\n\nPlease verify your connection."}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </div>

          <AlertDialogFooter className="mt-2">
            <AlertDialogAction 
              onClick={() => setAlertMessage(null)}
              className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-white font-black uppercase text-sm tracking-wider h-11 px-8 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none w-full sm:w-auto"
            >
              Acknowledge
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
          
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { mapStudioActions } from "@/lib/actions/mapStudio";
import { DownloadCloud, FileJson, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

export function DataExport() {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);

  const handleExport = async () => {
    if (!activeOrgId) return;
    setIsExporting(true);
    try {
      const blob = await mapStudioActions.downloadExportZip(Number(activeOrgId));
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      a.download = `Backup.zip`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      document.body.removeChild(a);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Export failed", error);
      alert("Failed to export maps. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card className="rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden border-l-12 border-l-zinc-950 dark:border-l-zinc-100">
      <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 p-5">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600" />
          Data Compliance & Export
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mt-1">
          Generate master backups of spatial graphs and hardware ledgers.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      
      <CardContent className="p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-[radial-gradient(#e5e7eb_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-size-[16px_16px]">
          
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0  dark:shadow-none">
              <FileJson className="h-6 w-6 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h4 className="font-black text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase tracking-tight">Master System Backup</h4>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-500 mt-1 max-w-md leading-relaxed uppercase tracking-wider">
                Download JSON bundle: <span className="font-mono text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-300">.ECHONAV</span> files, Adjacency Lists, and Hardware Bindings.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Button 
            onClick={handleExport}
            disabled={isExporting}
            className="w-full sm:w-auto shrink-0 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 font-black uppercase tracking-wider h-12 px-6  dark:shadow-none transition-all active:translate-y-0.5 active:translate-x-0.5 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            <DownloadCloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            {isExporting ? "Exporting..." : "Trigger Export"}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Building2, Save, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider"; 
import { useGetOrganisation, useUpdateOrganisation } from "@/hooks/api/useAuth";
import { OrganisationDto, UpdateOrgRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";

export function InstitutionalProfile() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  
  // Pulling the ID from your team's specific metadata structure
  const activeOrgId = user?.user_metadata?.organization_id 
    ? parseInt(user.user_metadata.organization_id)  
    : undefined;

  const { data: orgData, isLoading } = useGetOrganisation(activeOrgId ?? 0);
  const { mutate: updateOrg, isPending } = useUpdateOrganisation();

  if (isLoading || activeOrgId === undefined) {
    return <div className="p-8 text-center text-zinc-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-sm">Loading profile...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <ProfileForm 
      activeOrgId={activeOrgId} 
      initialData={orgData} 
      updateOrg={updateOrg} 
      isPending={isPending} 
    />
  );
}

interface ProfileFormProps {
  activeOrgId: number;
  initialData?: OrganisationDto;
  updateOrg: (variables: { orgId: number; payload: UpdateOrgRequest }) => void;
  isPending: boolean;
}

function ProfileForm({ activeOrgId, initialData, updateOrg, isPending }: ProfileFormProps) {
  const [orgName, setOrgName] = useState(initialData?.orgName ?? "");
  const [adminName, setAdminName] = useState(initialData?.adminName ?? "");
  const [contactNo, setContactNo] = useState(initialData?.contactNo ?? "");
  const [address, setAddress] = useState(initialData?.address ?? "");

  const handleSave = () => {
    updateOrg({
      orgId: activeOrgId,
      payload: { orgName, contactNo, address, adminName }
    });
  };

  return (
    <Card className="rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 overflow-hidden">
      <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 p-5">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100" />
          Institutional Profile
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-500 mt-1">
          Update registered details and infrastructure defaults.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      
      <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <Label className="text-[11px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Institution Name</Label>
            <Input value={orgName} onChange={(e) => setOrgName(e.target.value)} className="h-11 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 font-bold focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:ring-offset-0 focus-visible:border-zinc-950" />
          </div>
          
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <Label className="text-[11px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Admin Name</Label>
            <Input value={adminName} onChange={(e) => setAdminName(e.target.value)} className="h-11 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 font-bold focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:ring-offset-0 focus-visible:border-zinc-950" />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <Label className="text-[11px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Contact Number</Label>
            <Input value={contactNo} onChange={(e) => setContactNo(e.target.value)} className="h-11 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 font-bold focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:ring-offset-0 focus-visible:border-zinc-950" />
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <Label className="text-[11px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Organization Address</Label>
            <Input value={address} onChange={(e) => setAddress(e.target.value)} className="h-11 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 font-bold focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:ring-offset-0 focus-visible:border-zinc-950" />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-4 flex justify-end">
          <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={isPending} className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-black uppercase tracking-wider h-12 px-8  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed">
            {isPending ? <><Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> Saving...</> : <><Save className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Save Profile</>}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { ShieldCheck, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";

export interface ActiveStaff {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: "ADMIN" | "TECHNICIAN";
  joinedDate: string;
}

interface ActiveStaffRosterProps {
  staff: ActiveStaff[];
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
}

export function ActiveStaffRoster({ staff, onDelete }: ActiveStaffRosterProps) {
  return (
    <Card className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl overflow-hidden h-full flex flex-col">
      <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 py-5">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-black flex items-center gap-3 uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-1050 dark:text-emerald-500">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
          Active Staff Roster
        </CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="p-0 overflow-x-auto flex-1">
        <div className="min-w-[700px]">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-[1.2fr_1.5fr_1fr_0.8fr_80px] gap-4 px-6 py-4 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-black text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-widest bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50">
            <span>Name</span>
            <span>Email</span>
            <span>Role</span>
            <span className="text-right">Joined</span>
            <span className="text-center">Remove</span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col">

              <div className="px-6 py-8 text-center text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest">
                No active staff found.
              </div>
            ) : (
              staff.map((member, index) => {
                const isLast = index === staff.length - 1;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={member.id}
                    className={`grid grid-cols-[1.2fr_1.5fr_1fr_0.8fr_80px] gap-4 px-6 py-5 items-center hover:bg-emerald-300/20 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/20 transition-colors ${!isLast ? "border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800" : ""}`}
                  >
                    <span className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 truncate">
                      {member.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 truncate tracking-wide">
                      {member.email}
                    </span>
                    <div>
                      <Badge
                        variant="default"
                        className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest border px-2 py-1 ${member.role === "ADMIN"
                            ? "bg-indigo-100 border-indigo-300 text-indigo-900 dark:bg-indigo-900/50 dark:border-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-100"
                            : "bg-zinc-100 border-zinc-300 text-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                          }`}
                      >
                        {member.role}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 text-right uppercase tracking-wider">
                      {member.joinedDate}
                    </span>

                    {/* Delete Action Button */}
                    <div className="flex justify-center">
                      <AlertDialog>
                        <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                          <button className="p-2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-red-600..." title="Remove">
                            <Trash2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                          </button>
                        </AlertDialogTrigger>
                        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl">
                          <AlertDialogHeader>
                            <AlertDialogTitle className="font-black uppercase tracking-tight">Confirm Deletion</AlertDialogTitle>
                            <AlertDialogDescription className="font-bold text-zinc-500">
                              Are you sure you want to remove this record? This action cannot be undone.
                            </AlertDialogDescription>
                          </AlertDialogHeader>
                          <AlertDialogFooter>
                            <AlertDialogCancel className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 font-bold uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10  dark:shadow-none">
                              Cancel
                            </AlertDialogCancel>
                            <AlertDialogAction
                              onClick={() => onDelete(member.id)}
                              className="bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-black uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider h-10 border-2 border-zinc-950  dark:shadow-none"
                            >
                              Delete
                            </AlertDialogAction>

                        </AlertDialogContent>
                      </AlertDialog>

                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}

        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Clock, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,

  AlertDialogFooter,

  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger

export interface PendingInvite {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  inviteCode: string;
  status: "PENDING" | "EXPIRED";
  expirationDate: string;
}

interface InvitationQueueProps {
  invites: PendingInvite[];
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
}

export function InvitationQueue({ invites, onDelete }: InvitationQueueProps) {
  return (
    <Card className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl overflow-hidden h-full flex flex-col">

            <span>Expires</span>
            <span className="text-right">Status</span>

                    </span>

                            ? "bg-amber-200 text-amber-900 border-amber-400 dark:bg-amber-900/50 dark:text-amber-100 dark:border-amber-700"
                            : "bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 shadow-none"
                          }`}
                      >
                        {invite.status}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-center">
                      <AlertDialog>
                        <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                          <button className="p-2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-red-600..." title="Remove">
                            <Trash2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                          </button>

                        <AlertDialogContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl">

                              Cancel

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  Select,
  SelectContent,
  SelectItem,
  SelectTrigger,
  SelectValue,
} from "@/components/ui/select";
import { useInviteStaff } from "@/hooks/api/useStaff";
import { Mail, Send, UserPlus } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";

export function InviteEngine() {
  const { activeOrgId, user } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("technician");
  const [alertMessage, setAlertMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const inviteMutation = useInviteStaff();

  const handleClearAlert = () => {
    setAlertMessage("");
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeOrgId || !user?.id || !email) return;

    try {
      await inviteMutation.mutateAsync({
        email,
        role: role.toUpperCase(),
        organisationId: Number(activeOrgId),
        invitedById: Number(user.id),
      });
      // Clear the form on success
      setEmail("");
      setAlertMessage("Invitation sent successfully!");
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) alert(error.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    // THE FIX: Removed 'min-h-screen p-6' so it sizes naturally
    <div className="relative">
      <Card className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950  dark:shadow-none overflow-hidden rounded-xl transition-all">
        <CardHeader className="border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-indigo-300 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 py-5">
          <CardTitle className="text-sm font-black flex items-center gap-3 uppercase tracking-widest text-zinc-950 dark:text-indigo-500">
            <UserPlus className="h-5 w-5" />
            Staff Invitation Engine
          </CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-8">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-6">

              {/* Input Grid Layout */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

                {/* Role Selection */}
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  <label className="text-xs font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-widest">
                    Assign Role
                  </label>
                  <Select value={role} onValueChange={setRole}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="w-full border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 focus:ring-0 focus:ring-offset-0 focus:border-indigo-400 dark:focus:border-indigo-500 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 h-12 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none transition-all">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Select role" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent className="border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none font-bold">
                      <SelectItem value="technician">Technician</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="admin">Admin</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* Email Input */}
                <div className="space-y-3 sm:col-span-2">
                  <label className="text-xs font-black text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-widest">
                    Email Address
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-5 w-5 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400" />
                    <Input
                      type="email"
                      placeholder="staff.name@institution.edu"
                      className="pl-12 w-full border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 focus-visible:ring-0 focus-visible:border-indigo-400 dark:focus-visible:border-indigo-500 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 h-12 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg  dark:shadow-none transition-all"
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                      disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Footer & Action Area */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-4">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-center sm:text-left">
                  * Invites expire automatically after 48 hours for security compliance.
                </p>
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={!email || inviteMutation.isPending}
                  className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-bold h-11 px-6 dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none shrink-0 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Send className="h-4 w-4 mr-3" />
                  {inviteMutation.isPending ? "Sending..." : "Generate & Send Invite"}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      <AlertDialog open={!!alertMessage} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && handleClearAlert()}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Notification</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              {alertMessage}
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={handleClearAlert}>
              Acknowledge
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}
