"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { useInviteUser } from "@/hooks/api/useUsers";
import { KeyRound, MailCheck, X } from "lucide-react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { InvitedUsers } from "./InvitedUserList";

interface InviteNewUserProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSendInvite: (newUser: InvitedUsers) => void;
  pairingPin: string; 
}

export function InviteNewUser({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}: InviteNewUserProps) {
  const { activeOrgId, user } = useAuth();
  const inviteMutation = useInviteUser();
  const [successStatus, setSuccessStatus] = useState<"EMAILED" | "CONCIERGE" | null>(null);
  const [generatedPin, setGeneratedPin] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isConcierge, setIsConcierge] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleClose = () => {
    setSuccessStatus(null);
    setGeneratedPin(null);
    setIsConcierge(false);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();

    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    const firstName = String(formData.get("firstName") ?? "").trim();
    const lastName = String(formData.get("lastName") ?? "").trim();
    const email = String(formData.get("email") ?? "").trim();

    if (!firstName || !lastName || !email || !activeOrgId || !user?.id) return;

    try {
      const response = await inviteMutation.mutateAsync({
        firstName,
        lastName,
        email,
        organisationId: Number(activeOrgId),
        generatedById: Number(user.id),
        requiresConcierge: isConcierge,
      });
      
      if (response.pairingPin === "EMAILED") {
        setSuccessStatus("EMAILED");
      } else {
        setSuccessStatus("CONCIERGE");
        setGeneratedPin(response.pairingPin);
      }

    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) alert(error.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-zinc-950/60 dark:bg-zinc-950/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800  dark:shadow-none rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center px-6 py-5 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50">
          <h2 className="font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-lg text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2">
            {successStatus ? <MailCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" /> : <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5" />}
            {successStatus ? "Access Granted" : "Invite A New User"}
          </h2>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClose}
            className="text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-6 h-6" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {successStatus === "EMAILED" && (
          <div className="p-8 space-y-8 flex flex-col items-center">
             <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/30">
              <p className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-center">
                Access Token Dispatched
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 text-center mt-3">
                An email containing the 6-digit access PIN has been successfully sent to the user.
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button onClick={handleClose} className="w-full bg-zinc-950 text-white font-bold h-11 uppercase tracking-widest">
              Close Window
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {successStatus === "CONCIERGE" && (
          <div className="p-6 space-y-8 flex flex-col items-center">
             <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full bg-[radial-gradient(#e5e7eb_1px,transparent_1px)] dark:bg-[radial-gradient(#27272a_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-size-[16px_16px] p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
              <p className="text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4 text-center">
                Instruct the user to enter this PIN in their EchoNav mobile app. This code will self-destruct from the dashboard once used.
              </p>

              <div className="bg-zinc-950 dark:bg-zinc-100 text-white dark:text-zinc-950 w-full py-6 rounded-xl border-4 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-100 shadow-[6px_6px_0px_0px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] mb-2 flex justify-center">
                <span className="text-5xl sm:text-6xl font-mono font-black tracking-[0.2em] ml-4">
                  {generatedPin}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Button onClick={handleClose} className="w-full bg-zinc-950 text-white font-bold h-11 uppercase tracking-widest">
              Done
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {!successStatus && (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="p-6 space-y-8">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="first-name">Name</Label>
                    <Input
                      id="first-name"
                      name="firstName"
                      placeholder="Elena"
                      className="h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 dark:bg-zinc-900 focus-visible:ring-primary/20 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 font-medium"
                      required
                      disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="last-name">Surname</Label>
                    <Input
                      id="last-name"
                      name="lastName"
                      placeholder="Rostova"
                      className="h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 dark:bg-zinc-900 focus-visible:ring-primary/20 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 font-medium"
                      required
                      disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="user-email">Email Address</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="user-email"
                    name="email"
                    type="email"
                    placeholder="student@institution.edu"
                    className="h-11 rounded-lg bg-secondary/30 dark:bg-zinc-900 focus-visible:ring-primary/20 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 font-medium"
                    required
                    disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 pt-2">
                  <label className="relative flex cursor-pointer items-center">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      className="peer sr-only"
                      checked={isConcierge}
                      onChange={(e) => setIsConcierge(e.target.checked)}
                      disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                    />
                    <div className="h-6 w-11 rounded-full bg-zinc-200 border-2 border-zinc-300 peer-checked:bg-zinc-950 peer-checked:border-zinc-950 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:peer-checked:bg-zinc-100 dark:peer-checked:border-zinc-100 after:absolute after:top-[4px] after:left-[4px] after:h-4 after:w-4 after:rounded-full after:bg-white dark:after:bg-zinc-400 peer-checked:after:translate-x-[20px] dark:peer-checked:after:bg-zinc-950 after:transition-all"></div>
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex flex-col">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-white uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Requires Concierge Assistance
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-zinc-500">
                      Show PIN on screen for visually impaired users.
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 border-t-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={handleClose}
                disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                className="font-bold border-2 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 h-11"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>

              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={inviteMutation.isPending}
                className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white font-bold h-11  dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none"
              >
                {inviteMutation.isPending ? "Generating..." : "Generate & Dispatch PIN"}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useAuth } from "@/components/layout/auth-provider";
import { useDeleteUser, useGetPendingUserPins, useGetUsers } from "@/hooks/api/useUsers";
import { KeyRound } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "../ui/button";
import { ActiveUserList, ActiveUsers } from "./ActiveUserList";
import { InvitedUsers, InvitedUsersList } from "./InvitedUserList";
import { InviteNewUser } from "./InviteNewUser";

export function UserComposer() {
  const { activeOrgId } = useAuth();
  const orgIdNum = activeOrgId ? Number(activeOrgId) : undefined;

  const { data: usersDtoList, isLoading, isError } = useGetUsers(orgIdNum);
  const { data: pinsDtoList, isLoading: pinsLoading } = useGetPendingUserPins(orgIdNum);
  const deleteUserMutation = useDeleteUser(orgIdNum);

  const [isInviteOpen, setIsInviteOpen] = useState(false);

  const activeUsers: ActiveUsers[] = (usersDtoList || []).map((user) => ({
    id: String(user.id),
    name: `${user.firstName} ${user.lastName}`.trim(),
    email: user.email,
    pin: "******", 
    generatedBy: "System Admin",
    joinedDate: "Recently",
  }));

  const invitedUsers: InvitedUsers[] = (pinsDtoList || []).map((pin) => ({
    id: String(pin.id),
    name: `${pin.firstName} ${pin.lastName}`.trim(),
    email: pin.email,
    pin: pin.pinCode, 
    generatedBy: pin.generatedBy,
    invitedDate: new Date(pin.invitedDate).toLocaleDateString(),
  }));

  const handleDeleteActiveUser = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteUserMutation.mutateAsync(Number(id));
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) alert(error.message);
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteInvitedUser = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteUserMutation.mutateAsync(Number(id));
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (error instanceof Error) alert(error.message);
    }
  };

  const handleAddInvitedUser = (newUser: InvitedUsers) => {
    console.log("New user invited:", newUser);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 pb-12">
      <div className="flex flex-col min-h-full">
        <div className="sticky -top-6 md:-top-14 z-40 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-b-2 border-zinc-950 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 -mt-6 md:-mt-8 -mx-6 md:-mx-8 px-6 md:px-8 pt-6 md:pt-8 pb-5 mb-8">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-100 uppercase">
              User Management
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">
              Invite new users and view current and pending users.
            </p>
          </div>

          <Button
            onClick={() => setIsInviteOpen(true)}
            className="bg-zinc-950 hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:text-zinc-950 text-white rounded-lg font-bold h-11 px-6 dark:shadow-none transition-transform active:translate-y-1 active:translate-x-1 active:shadow-none shrink-0"
          >
            <KeyRound className="w-5 h-5 mr-2" />
            Invite A New User
          </Button>
        </div>

        {isLoading && (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-zinc-500 font-bold animate-pulse uppercase tracking-widest border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-300 rounded-xl">
            Loading User Directory...
          </div>
        )}

        {isError && (
          <div className="p-8 text-center text-red-700 bg-red-50 border-2 border-red-500 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl">
            Failed to load user directory. Please check your connection.
          </div>
        )}

        {!isLoading && !isError && (
          <div className="max-w-full space-y-8">
            <ActiveUserList
              users={activeUsers}
              onDelete={handleDeleteActiveUser}
            />
            <InvitedUsersList
              users={invitedUsers}
              onDelete={handleDeleteInvitedUser}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <InviteNewUser
        isOpen={isInviteOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsInviteOpen(false)}
        onSendInvite={handleAddInvitedUser}
        pairingPin="" 
      />
    </div>
  );
}

// Centralized Query Key Factory
export const queryKeys = {
  mapStudio: {
    buildings: (orgId?: number) => ["buildings", orgId] as const,
    floorPlans: (buildingId?: number) => ["floorPlans", buildingId] as const,
    spatialGraph: (floorPlanId?: number) => ["spatialGraph", floorPlanId] as const,
    buildingStats: (buildingId?: number) => ["buildingStats", buildingId] as const,
    floorPlanStats: (floorPlanId?: number) => ["floorPlanStats", floorPlanId] as const,
  },
  fleet: {
    staffTasks: (staffId?: number) => ["staffTasks", staffId] as const,
  },
  staff: {
    roster: (orgId?: number) => ["staff", "roster", orgId] as const,
  },
  users: {
    roster: (orgId?: number) => ["users", "roster", orgId] as const,
  },
  auth: {
    session: ["auth", "session"] as const,
  },
  dashboard: {
    data: (orgId?: number) => ["dashboard", orgId] as const,
  },
};

import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { authActions } from "@/lib/actions/auth";
import { UpdateOrgRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";

export const useGetOrganisation = (orgId: number) => {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["organisation", orgId],
    queryFn: () => authActions.getOrganisation(orgId),
    enabled: !!orgId, // Only fetch if we actually have an ID
  });
};

export const useUpdateOrganisation = () => {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ orgId, payload }: { orgId: number; payload: UpdateOrgRequest }) =>
      authActions.updateOrganisation(orgId, payload),
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["organisation", variables.orgId] });
    },
  });
};

import { fleetActions } from "@/lib/actions/fleet";
import { CreateBatchTasksRequest, LinkBeaconRequest, UpdateTaskRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { queryKeys } from "./queryKeys";

export function useGetStaffTasks(staffId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.fleet.staffTasks(staffId),
    queryFn: () => fleetActions.getStaffTasks(staffId!),
    enabled: !!staffId && !isNaN(staffId),
  });
}

export function useUpdateTaskStatus() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ taskId, data }: { taskId: number; data: UpdateTaskRequest }) => 
      fleetActions.updateTaskStatus(taskId, data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      // Invalidate both the specific staff tasks and the global tasks
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staffTasks"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["tasks"] });
    },
  });
}

export function useGetOrganizationTasks(orgId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["tasks", orgId],
    queryFn: () => fleetActions.getOrganizationTasks(orgId!),
    enabled: !!orgId && !isNaN(orgId),
  });
}

export function useGetOrganizationBeacons(orgId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["beacons", orgId],
    queryFn: () => fleetActions.getOrganizationBeacons(orgId!),
    enabled: !!orgId && !isNaN(orgId),
  });
}

// --- FIX: Robust Cache Invalidation for Linking ---
export function useLinkBeacon() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: LinkBeaconRequest) => fleetActions.linkBeacon(data),
    onSuccess: () => {
      // Instantly refresh the inventory table the second a beacon is linked
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["beacons"] });
    }
  });
}

export function useCreateTasks() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: CreateBatchTasksRequest) => fleetActions.createTasks(data),
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.fleet.staffTasks(variables.staffId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["tasks"] }); 
    },
  });
}

import {

    UpdateGraphRequest
} from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { queryKeys } from "./queryKeys";

// --- BUILDINGS ---

export function useGetBuildings(orgId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.mapStudio.buildings(orgId),
    queryFn: () => mapStudioActions.getBuildings(orgId!),
    enabled: !!orgId && !isNaN(orgId),
  });
}

export function useCreateBuilding() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ orgId, data }: { orgId: number; data: RegisterBuildingRequest }) => 
      mapStudioActions.createBuilding(orgId, data),
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.mapStudio.buildings(variables.orgId) });
    },
  });

import { staffActions } from "@/lib/actions/staff";
import { InviteStaffRequest } from "@/lib/api/types";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { queryKeys } from "./queryKeys";

export function useGetStaff(orgId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.staff.roster(orgId),
    queryFn: () => staffActions.getStaff(orgId!),
    enabled: !!orgId && !isNaN(orgId),
    // SMART POLLING: Refreshes active roster every 10 seconds
    refetchInterval: 10000, 
  });
}

export function useInviteStaff() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (data: InviteStaffRequest) => staffActions.inviteStaff(data),
    onSuccess: (_, variables) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.staff.roster(variables.organisationId) });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff", "invites", variables.organisationId] });
    },
  });
}

export function useGetPendingInvites(orgId: number | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["staff", "invites", orgId],
    queryFn: () => staffActions.getPendingInvites(orgId!),
    enabled: !!orgId && !isNaN(orgId),
    // SMART POLLING: Aggressively checks for invite acceptance every 3 seconds
    refetchInterval: 3000,
  });
}

export function useDeleteStaff(orgId: number | undefined) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (staffId: number) => staffActions.deleteStaff(staffId),
    onSuccess: () => {
      if (orgId) {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.staff.roster(orgId) });
      } else {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff"] });
      }
    },
  });
}

export function useDeleteStaffInvite(orgId: number | undefined) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (inviteId: number) => staffActions.deleteStaffInvite(inviteId),
    onSuccess: () => {
      if (orgId) {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff", "invites", orgId] });
      } else {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["staff", "invites"] });
      }
    },
  });
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\hooks\AutoAddressGoogleMaps.ts
====================================================================================================

"use client";

import { RefObject, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

export function useGooglePlacesAutocomplete(
  inputRef: RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>,
  countryCode: string | undefined | null,
  onAddressSelect: (details: any) => void,
) {
  const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<Error | null>(null);
  const autocompleteRef = useRef<google.maps.places.Autocomplete | null>(null);

  // 1. Load Google Maps Script
  useEffect(() => {
    // Short-circuit if there's no API key configured
    if (!process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY) {
      setLoadError(new Error("Google Maps API key is missing."));
      return;
    }

    // If it's already loaded and ready
    if (window.google?.maps?.places) {
      setIsLoaded(true);
      return;
    }

    // Set up the global callback that Google Maps will look for
    (window as any).initGooglePlaces = () => {
      setIsLoaded(true);
    };

    const existingScript = document.querySelector(
      'script[src*="maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js"]',
    );

    if (existingScript) {
      // If the script tag is there, we just wait for the callback to fire.
      return;
    }

    const script = document.createElement("script");
    script.src = `https://maps.googleapis.com/maps/api/js?key=${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_MAPS_API_KEY}&libraries=places&loading=async&callback=initGooglePlaces`;
    script.async = true;
    script.defer = true;
    
    script.onerror = () => {
      setLoadError(new Error("Google Maps script failed to load."));
    };
    
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  }, []);

  // 2. Initialize Autocomplete UI on the Input
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isLoaded || !inputRef.current || loadError) return;

    try {
      autocompleteRef.current = new google.maps.places.Autocomplete(inputRef.current, {
        fields: ["address_components", "geometry"],
        componentRestrictions: countryCode ? { country: countryCode.toLowerCase() } : undefined,
      });

      const listener = autocompleteRef.current.addListener("place_changed", () => {
        const place = autocompleteRef.current?.getPlace();
        
        if (!place || !place.address_components) return;

        let streetNumber = "";
        let route = "";
        let city = "";
        let stateProvince = "";
        let postalCode = "";
        let selectedCountryCode = "";

        for (const component of place.address_components) {
          const types = component.types;
          if (types.includes("street_number")) streetNumber = component.long_name;
          if (types.includes("route")) route = component.long_name;
          if (types.includes("locality") || types.includes("postal_town")) city = component.long_name;
          if (types.includes("administrative_area_level_1")) stateProvince = component.short_name;
          if (types.includes("postal_code")) postalCode = component.long_name;
          if (types.includes("country")) selectedCountryCode = component.short_name;
        }

        onAddressSelect({
          streetAddress: `${streetNumber} ${route}`.trim(),
          city,
          stateProvince,
          postalCode,
          countryCode: selectedCountryCode,
        });
      });

      return () => {
        if (listener) {
          google.maps.event.removeListener(listener);
        }
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Google Places Autocomplete failed to initialize. Falling back to manual entry.", err);
      setLoadError(err instanceof Error ? err : new Error("Unknown initialization error"));
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isLoaded, inputRef, loadError]); 

  // 3. Dynamically update country restrictions
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      if (autocompleteRef.current && countryCode) {
        autocompleteRef.current.setComponentRestrictions({
          country: countryCode.toLowerCase(),
        });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("Failed to update component restrictions", err);
    }
  }, [countryCode]);

  return { isLoaded, loadError };
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\hooks\use-mobile.ts
====================================================================================================

import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange)
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}

import { axiosInstance } from "@/lib/api/axios";
import {
  ActivateInviteRequest,
  LoginResponse,
  MobileSignupRequest,
  PairingRequest,
  RegisterOrgRequest,
  UpdateOrgRequest,
  OrganisationDto
} from "@/lib/api/types";
import { AxiosError } from "axios";

export const authActions = {
  register: async (payload: RegisterOrgRequest): Promise<string> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post("/api/auth/register", payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Registration failed.");
    }
  },

  login: async (email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post<LoginResponse>("/api/auth/login", { email, password });
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Invalid credentials.");
    }
  },

  pairDevice: async (payload: PairingRequest): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post<LoginResponse>("/api/auth/pair", payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Invalid pairing PIN.");
    }
  },

  mobileSignup: async (payload: MobileSignupRequest): Promise<LoginResponse> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post<LoginResponse>("/api/auth/mobile-signup", payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Mobile signup failed.");
    }
  },

  // THE FIX: Added the activation action
  activateInvite: async (token: string, payload: ActivateInviteRequest): Promise<string> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post(`/api/auth/activate/${token}`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Activation failed.");
    }
  },

  logout: async (): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await axiosInstance.post("/api/auth/logout");
    } catch {
      console.error("Server logout failed, clearing local state anyway.");
    }
  },

  // --- NEW: Fetch Organisation Details ---
  getOrganisation: async (orgId: number): Promise<OrganisationDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<OrganisationDto>(`/api/auth/organisations/${orgId}`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch organisation details.");
    }
  },

  // --- NEW: Update Organisation ---
  updateOrganisation: async (orgId: number, payload: UpdateOrgRequest): Promise<OrganisationDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.put<OrganisationDto>(`/api/auth/organisations/${orgId}`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to update organisation.");
    }
  }
};

import { axiosInstance } from "@/lib/api/axios";
import {
  BuildingDto,
  FloorPlanDto,
  GraphDto,
  NodeDto,
  RegisterBuildingRequest,
  UpdateBuildingPrivacyRequest,
  UpdateFloorPlanRequest,
  UpdateGraphRequest,
  BuildingStatsDto, // <-- Added
  FloorPlanStatsDto // <-- Added
} from "@/lib/api/types";
import { AxiosError } from "axios";

export const mapStudioActions = {
  // --- BUILDINGS ---
  createBuilding: async (orgId: number, payload: RegisterBuildingRequest): Promise<BuildingDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post<BuildingDto>(`/api/mapstudio/organisations/${orgId}/buildings`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to create building.");
    }
  },

  deleteBuilding: async (buildingId: number): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await axiosInstance.delete(`/api/mapstudio/buildings/${buildingId}`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to delete building.");
    }
  },

  getBuildings: async (orgId: number): Promise<BuildingDto[]> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<BuildingDto[]>(`/api/mapstudio/organisations/${orgId}/buildings`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch buildings.");
    }
  },

  updateBuildingPrivacy: async (buildingId: number, payload: UpdateBuildingPrivacyRequest): Promise<BuildingDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.patch<BuildingDto>(`/api/mapstudio/buildings/${buildingId}/privacy`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to update building privacy.");
    }
  },

  // --- FLOOR PLANS ---
  getFloorPlans: async (buildingId: number): Promise<FloorPlanDto[]> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<FloorPlanDto[]>(`/api/mapstudio/buildings/${buildingId}/floorplans`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch floor plans.");
    }
  },

  updateFloorPlan: async (floorPlanId: number, payload: UpdateFloorPlanRequest): Promise<FloorPlanDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.put<FloorPlanDto>(`/api/mapstudio/floorplans/${floorPlanId}`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to update floor plan.");
    }
  },

  deleteFloorPlan: async (floorPlanId: number): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await axiosInstance.delete(`/api/mapstudio/floorplans/${floorPlanId}`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to delete floor plan.");
    }
  },

  uploadFloorPlan: async (buildingId: number, name: string, file: File) => {
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("name", name);
      formData.append("file", file);

      const res = await axiosInstance.post(`/api/mapstudio/buildings/${buildingId}/floorplans/upload`, formData, {
        headers: { "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data" },
      });
      return res.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message?: string }>;
      const rawData = axiosError.response?.data;
      const errorMessage = typeof rawData === "string" ? rawData : rawData?.message;
      
      throw new Error(errorMessage || "Failed to upload floor plan.");
    }
  },

  downloadExportZip: async (orgId: number) => {
    // FIXED: Using axiosInstance instead of api
    const res = await axiosInstance.get(`/api/exportmap/organisations/${orgId}/export`, {
      responseType: 'blob'
    });
    return res.data;
  },

  // --- SPATIAL GRAPHS ---
  getSpatialGraph: async (floorPlanId: number): Promise<GraphDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<GraphDto>(`/api/mapstudio/floorplans/${floorPlanId}/graph`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch spatial graph.");
    }
  },

  updateSpatialGraph: async (floorPlanId: number, payload: UpdateGraphRequest): Promise<GraphDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.put<GraphDto>(`/api/mapstudio/floorplans/${floorPlanId}/graph`, payload);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to save vector graph.");
    }
  },

  deleteNode: async (nodeId: number): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await axiosInstance.delete(`/api/mapstudio/nodes/${nodeId}`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to delete node.");
    }
  },

  // --- TTS INTEGRATION ---
  generateNodeTts: async (nodeId: number, text: string): Promise<NodeDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.post<NodeDto>(`/api/mapstudio/nodes/${nodeId}/tts/generate`, { text });
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to generate TTS audio.");
    }
  },

  deleteNodeTts: async (nodeId: number): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await axiosInstance.delete(`/api/mapstudio/nodes/${nodeId}/tts`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to delete TTS audio.");
    }
  },

  // --- STATS (TICKET 8) ---
  getBuildingStats: async (buildingId: number): Promise<BuildingStatsDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<BuildingStatsDto>(`/api/mapstudio/buildings/${buildingId}/stats`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch building stats.");
    }
  },

  getFloorPlanStats: async (floorPlanId: number): Promise<FloorPlanStatsDto> => {
    try {
      const response = await axiosInstance.get<FloorPlanStatsDto>(`/api/mapstudio/floorplans/${floorPlanId}/stats`);
      return response.data;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const axiosError = err as AxiosError<{ message: string }>;
      throw new Error(axiosError.response?.data?.message || "Failed to fetch floor plan stats.");
    }
  }
};

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\lib\api\axios.ts
====================================================================================================

import axios from 'axios';

export const axiosInstance = axios.create({
  baseURL: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8080',
});

// Intercept all outgoing requests and attach the Bearer token dynamically
axiosInstance.interceptors.request.use(
  (config) => {
    // THE FIX: Strict Client-Side Cookie Parsing.
    // Removes the dynamic `next/headers` import that causes Webpack/SSR compilation crashes.
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const match = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(^| )echonav_token=([^;]+)'));
      if (match) {
        config.headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${match[2]}`;
      }
    }
    return config;
  },
  (error) => Promise.reject(error)
);

// ============================================================================
// ECHONAV DOMAIN TYPES
// Single Source of Truth for Frontend/Backend Data Contracts
// ============================================================================

// --- USER MANAGEMENT DOMAIN ---

export interface UserDto {
  id: number;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
  // Based on Phase 1, the backend returns the raw NavigatingUser entity/DTO
}

export interface InviteUserRequest {
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
  organisationId: number;
  generatedById: number;
  requiresConcierge: boolean;
}

export interface InviteUserResponse {
  pairingPin: string;
}

// --- AUTH DOMAIN ---

export interface LoginResponse {
  token: string;
  role: string;
  organisationId: number | null;
  userId: number;
}

export interface RegisterOrgRequest {
  orgName: string;
  adminName: string;
  email: string;
  password?: string; // Optional on frontend if handling via separate state, but required by backend
  country: string;
  address: string;
  contactNo: string;
}

export interface UpdateOrgRequest {
  orgName: string;
  contactNo: string;
  address: string;
  adminName: string; // <-- ADDED FOR PHASE 2
}

export interface OrganisationDto {
  id: number;
  orgName: string;
  adminName: string;
  orgEmail: string;
  slug: string;
  country: string;
  address: string;
  contactNo: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface MobileSignupRequest {
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
  password?: string;
}

export interface PairingRequest {
  email: string;
  pairingPin: string;
}

export interface ActivateInviteRequest {
  email: string;
  name: string;
  password?: string;
}

// --- MAP STUDIO DOMAIN ---

export interface BuildingDto {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  address: string;
  privacyLevel: "PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE";
}

export interface BuildingStatsDto {
  activeFloorPlans: number;
}

export interface FloorPlanStatsDto {
  nodeCount: number;
  edgeCount: number;
}

export interface RegisterBuildingRequest {
  name: string;
  address: string;
  privacyLevel: "PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE";
}

export interface UpdateBuildingPrivacyRequest {
  privacyLevel: "PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE";
}

export interface FloorPlanDto {
  id: number;
  buildingId: number | null;
  name: string;
  floorPlanUrl: string; // Updated from legacy floorPlanDWG
  scale: number | null;
  access: "PUBLIC" | "PRIVATE";
}

export interface UpdateFloorPlanRequest {
  name?: string;
  scale?: number;
}

export interface NodeDto {
  id: number;
  label: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  ttsVerbalGuidance?: string;
  ttsAudioUrl?: string;
  isHazard?: boolean;
  hazardType?: string;
  rssiBaseline?: number;
}

export interface EdgeDto {
  id: number;
  fromNodeId: number;
  toNodeId: number;
  weightM: number;
  distance: number;
  isHazard?: boolean;
}

export interface UpdateGraphRequest {
  nodes: {
    tempId: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    label: string;
    isHazard?: boolean;
    hazardType?: string;
    ttsVerbalGuidance?: string;
    ttsAudioUrl?: string;
  }[];
  edges: {
    tempId: string;
    source: string;
    target: string;
    isHazard?: boolean;
  }[];
}

export interface GraphDto {
  floorPlan: FloorPlanDto;
  nodes: NodeDto[];
  edges: EdgeDto[];
}

export interface GraphNodePayload {
  tempId: string;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  label: string;
  isHazard?: boolean;
  hazardType?: string; 
}

export interface NodeData { 
  id: string; 
  x: number; 
  y: number; 
  label: string;
  isHazard?: boolean; 
  hazardType?: string; 
}

export interface GraphEdgePayload {
  tempId: string;
  source: string;
  target: string;
}

// --- FLEET DOMAIN ---

export interface BeaconDto {
  id: number;
  nodeId: number | null;
  nodeLabel?: string | null; // <-- FIX: Added to sync with backend Phase 1 update
  uuid: string | null; // Mapped from Java UUID
  major: number | null;
  minor: number | null;
  calibratedRssiBaseline: number | null;
  status: string; // e.g., "ACTIVE", "UNLINKED"
  linkedDate: string | null; // Mapped from ZonedDateTime
}

export interface DeploymentTaskDto {
  id: number;
  nodeId: number | null;
  staffId: number | null;
  status: string; // e.g., "TO_DO", "IN_PROGRESS", "VERIFIED"
  timeAssigned: string | null; // Mapped from ZonedDateTime
  timeCompleted: string | null; // Mapped from ZonedDateTime
}

export interface CreateBatchTasksRequest {
  nodeIds: number[];
  staffId: number;
}

export interface LinkBeaconRequest {
  uuid: string; // Mapped from Java UUID
  nodeId: number;
}

export interface UpdateTaskRequest {
  status: string;
}

// --- NAVIGATION DOMAIN ---

export interface RouteRequest {
  floorPlanId: number;
  startNodeId: number;
  endNodeId: number;
}

export interface RouteResponse {
  path: NodeDto[];
  totalDistanceMeters: number;
  estimatedTimeSeconds: number;
  status: "SUCCESS" | "NO_PATH_FOUND";
}

// --- STAFF DOMAIN ---

export interface StaffDto {
  id: number;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  status: string;
  joinedDate?: string;
}

export interface InviteStaffRequest {
  email: string;
  role: string;
  organisationId: number;
  invitedById: number;
}

export interface StaffInviteDto {
  id: number;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  status: string; // "PENDING" | "EXPIRED"
  expirationDate: string;
}

export interface PairingPinDto {
  id: number;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
  pinCode: string; // The concealed ****** pin
  generatedBy: string;
  invitedDate: string;
}

// --- DASHBOARD DOMAIN ---

export interface StatMetric {
  count: number;
  subtext: string;
}

export interface DashboardStatsDto {
  mappedBuildings: StatMetric;
  activeBeacons: StatMetric;
  pendingTasks: StatMetric;
}

export interface RecentPlanDto {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  building: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface HardwareHealthDto {
  total: number;
  healthyCount: number;
  warningCount: number;
  criticalCount: number;
  criticalItems: unknown[];
}

export interface SystemEventDto {
  id: number;
  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'RESOLVED';
  hazardType: string;
  nodeLabel: string;
  buildingName: string;
  floorPlanName: string;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface DashboardResponseDto {
  userName: string;
  stats: DashboardStatsDto;
  recentPlans: RecentPlanDto[];
  healthData: HardwareHealthDto;
  systemEvents: SystemEventDto[];
  routingActivity: ActivityChartDto[];
}

export interface ActivityChartDto {
  date: string;
  users: number;
  hazards: number;
}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

FILE: C:\Users\bbdnet2832\Desktop\projects\echonav-web-testing-grounds\src\middleware.ts
====================================================================================================

import type { NextRequest } from 'next/server';
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

const protectedRoutes = ['/dashboard', '/map-studio', '/fleet', '/staff', '/user-management', '/settings'];
const authRoutes = ['/auth', '/reset-password'];

function parseJwt(token: string) {
  try {
    const base64Url = token.split('.')[1];
    const base64 = base64Url.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const jsonPayload = decodeURIComponent(atob(base64));
    return JSON.parse(jsonPayload);
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

export function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const tokenCookie = request.cookies.get('echonav_token')?.value;

  const isProtectedRoute = protectedRoutes.some(route => pathname.startsWith(route));
  const isAuthRoute = authRoutes.some(route => pathname.startsWith(route));

  // Block unauthorized users from protected routes
  if (isProtectedRoute && !tokenCookie) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/auth?tab=login', request.url));
  }

  if (tokenCookie) {
    const decoded = parseJwt(tokenCookie);
    
    // THE FIX: RBAC - Block Technicians from the Web Dashboard
    if (isProtectedRoute && decoded?.role === 'TECHNICIAN') {
      // Clear their token and kick them out. They belong on mobile.
      const response = NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/auth?tab=login&error=mobile_only', request.url));
      response.cookies.delete('echonav_token');
      return response;
    }

    // Redirect admins away from auth screens if already logged in
    if (isAuthRoute && decoded?.role !== 'TECHNICIAN') {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url));
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    '/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|sitemap.xml|robots.txt|.*\\.svg$).*)',
  ],
};
